Model: I have written request and proposed roster. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the employee self-service portal, then keep the submission receipt. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用僱員自助網上平台提交，之後保存網上確認資料。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 啲文件交齊之後，通常幾時先適合跟進呀？
Model: Once the documents are in, when would it be reasonable to follow up?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about five business days. During that period, keep your rosters, time records and pay slips, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約五個工作日。期間，保留更表、工時紀錄同糧單，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果未有決定之前我嘅聯絡資料變咗，我係咪更新返而家個申請，定係等有決定先？
Model: What should I do if my contact details change before there’s a decision, do I update the current request or wait for the decision first?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低彈性工作安排，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down flexible working arrangement so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D026 — Starting a new job
Topic: Employment

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a workplace relations adviser. I’m calling about new-job payroll paperwork. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
Model: 早晨。呢度係僱傭關係顧問。我聯絡你係關於新工出糧文件。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我最擔心工資或者僱傭待遇可能記錄錯。我想喺今個月底之前處理好。
Model: My concern is that my pay or employment entitlements may be recorded incorrectly. I’d like this resolved before the end of this month.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: This comes down to Tax File Number declaration. minimum entitlements can depend on the award, agreement and employment type. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.
Model: 而家要睇嘅重點係稅務檔案號碼申報。最低僱傭待遇可能視乎 award、協議同僱傭類別。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住第一次出糧日期係八月二十八日。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the first pay date is 28 August. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, ask payroll or the employer to review the matter in writing. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係以書面要求出糧部門或者僱主覆核。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗TFN 申報同退休金選擇表。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have TFN declaration and super choice form. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the employee self-service portal. Upload TFN declaration and super choice form, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用僱員自助網上平台。上載TFN 申報同退休金選擇表，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？
Model: If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.
Model: 重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如我覺得個決定有問題，係咪有正式嘅覆核程序可以跟？
Model: Suppose the decision doesn’t seem right, is there a formal review process I can use?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, raise it with the employer or payroll, then seek Fair Work information if needed.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，先向僱主或者出糧部門提出，有需要再向 Fair Work 查詢。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。
Model: Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: All right, I know what to do now. I’ll keep a copy of whatever I send.
Model: 好，而家我知點做喇。我交出去嘅資料都會留返副本。

## D027 — GP bulk billing
Topic: Health

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a clinic administration officer. I can see an enquiry about a GP appointment fee. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟診所行政職員。我見到有一個關於普通科醫生診症費嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到直接向 Medicare 收費。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions bulk billing, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. Your current record also says the clinic charges $78 with a Medicare rebate available.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。你目前紀錄亦顯示診所收七十八澳元，可以申請 Medicare 回贈。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺通知所列日期之前知道，因為治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the date shown on the notice, because treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗Medicare 卡同預約確認。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered Medicare card and appointment confirmation. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Have you brought copies of the main records?
Model: 主要紀錄嘅副本你有冇帶嚟？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 差唔多齊，我今晚可以攞埋欠嗰份。
Model: Nearly all. I can get the missing one this evening.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the clinic patient portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用診所病人網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我交咗啲文件之後，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
Model: Once I’ve submitted the documents, what happens while the matter is being checked?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about two weeks. While you wait, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約兩星期。等候期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果佢哋最後話唔得，我可唔可以睇咗理由先，再決定使唔使申請覆核？
Model: If they eventually say no, can I see the reasons before deciding whether to seek a review?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返直接向 Medicare 收費呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use bulk billing when I contact the service again.

## D028 — Specialist referral
Topic: Health

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a specialist referral. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進專科轉介。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係專科預約係十一月十八日。我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that the appointment is booked for 18 November. My concern is that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: For this matter, we need to be clear about specialist referral. In this type of matter, Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. Let me check how that applies to you.
Model: 決定下一步之前，要記住專科轉介信呢個詞。喺呢類事情，Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前都搞唔掂，問題會有幾大呀？
Model: How serious is it if I’m not able to fix this before the next scheduled appointment or payment?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有家庭醫生轉介信同專科預約資料。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will GP referral and specialist booking be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you access the evidence we just mentioned?
Model: 頭先提過嗰啲證明你而家開唔開到？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 差唔多全部有，不過仲有一樣冇帶喺身。
Model: I’ve got nearly everything, but one item isn’t with me.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the clinic patient portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經診所病人網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我正式提交之後，點知系統已經有紀錄，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。
Model: Once I lodge it, how will I know it’s on their system, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Note the lodgement reference. Allow about three to four weeks; during that time, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen.
Model: 保存網上確認資料。預大約三至四星期；期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如到時我仲係等緊，我係咪應該先問吓而家做到邊？
Model: Suppose I’m still waiting after that, should I ask for a progress update first?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住專科轉介信。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember specialist referral. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.
Model: 好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。

## D029 — Pathology test
Topic: Health

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain a pathology test request. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋病理化驗要求。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到驗血預約係星期一朝早，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that the blood test is booked for Monday morning, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Alright, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺下星期五之前處理。否則，我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
Model: I need to deal with it before next Friday. Otherwise, I’m worried that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: So the figure you gave me is still the one we should use?
Model: 咁頭先嗰個數字而家仲係用呢個，係咪？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 啱呀，如果有改我會再話你知。
Model: That’s right. I’ll let you know if it changes.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I see. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有化驗申請表同 Medicare 卡，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have request form and Medicare card, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Do you have copies of those records with you?
Model: 嗰啲紀錄你有冇副本喺身？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分有呀，不過仲有一份喺屋企。
Model: I have most of them; one is still at home.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the clinic patient portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經診所病人網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 件事仲處理緊嗰陣，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？
Model: While this is still being sorted out, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?
Model: 好，我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。
Model: Okay, Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將自付費用同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of out-of-pocket cost with the reference number for future contact.

## D030 — PBS prescription
Topic: Health

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of an expensive prescription medicine. I’ve found the enquiry. Before we get into the details, tell me what you need resolved.
Model: 我哋睇下昂貴處方藥嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我搵到你個查詢喇。講細節之前，你最想解決嘅係咩呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話處方係三個月份量。我仍然擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
Model: Earlier I was told the prescription is for a three-month supply. I’m still concerned that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme, or PBS. Remember that Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係藥物福利計劃，即係 PBS。要留意Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 有冇其他資料想我而家交，而唔係等過咗今個月底先補？
Model: Do you need anything else from me now, rather than after the end of this month?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗處方同 Medicare 資料。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have prescription and Medicare details. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the clinic patient portal, then keep the electronic acknowledgement. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用診所病人網上平台提交，之後留返電子確認紀錄。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 上載完成之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？
Model: After the upload is complete, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?
Model: 好，如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。
Model: Okay, Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低藥物福利計劃，即係 PBS，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down Pharmaceutical Benefits Scheme, or PBS so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D031 — Urgent care clinic
Topic: Health

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a clinic administration officer. I’m calling about same-day medical-care options. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
Model: 早晨。呢度係診所行政職員。我聯絡你係關於即日醫療選擇。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我最擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。我想喺通知所列日期之前處理好。
Model: My concern is that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost. I’d like this resolved before the date shown on the notice.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is Medicare Urgent Care Clinic. Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 呢件事最緊要先搞清楚Medicare 緊急護理診所。Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。我幫你睇吓你個情況實際點樣套用。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住症狀唔屬於危及生命。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the symptoms are not life-threatening. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗Medicare 卡同現用藥物清單。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have Medicare card and medication list. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the clinic patient portal. Upload Medicare card and medication list, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用診所病人網上平台。上載Medicare 卡同現用藥物清單，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 通常要等幾耐？我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
Model: How long should I wait? I’m worried that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
Model: 預大約兩星期。期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我懷疑個機構搞錯咗，我應該搵邊個再睇一次個決定？
Model: If I believe the service has made a mistake, who do I ask to look at the decision again?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白，我會留意訊息，收到任何新通知都會保存。
Model: Right, I’ll keep an eye on the messages and save any new notice I receive.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks. I’ll make sure I keep the receipt and a copy of the documents.
Model: 唔該。我會保存收據，同埋留返啲文件副本。

## D032 — Dental treatment quote
Topic: Health

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a clinic administration officer. I can see an enquiry about a dental treatment estimate. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟診所行政職員。我見到有一個關於牙科治療報價嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到治療計劃。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions treatment plan, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. Your current record also says the proposed crown costs $1,850.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。你目前紀錄亦顯示建議牙冠治療收一千八百五十澳元。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前知道，因為治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the next scheduled appointment or payment, because treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗書面報價同醫療保險資料。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered written quote and health-fund details. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you pull up those documents on your phone?
Model: 你可唔可以而家用電話開返啲文件？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要文件我可以畀你睇，仲有一份紀錄未搵到。
Model: I can show you the main documents; one record is missing.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the clinic patient portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用診所病人網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我正式交咗資料之後，等緊期間有冇啲嘢一定要做或者唔好做呀？
Model: After I lodge the information, is there anything I should or shouldn’t do while I’m waiting?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about three to four weeks. While you wait, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約三至四星期。等候期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果佢哋最後話唔得，我可唔可以叫佢哋講清楚點解，再決定係咪覆核？
Model: If they eventually say no, can I ask them to explain exactly why before I challenge it?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返治療計劃呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use treatment plan when I contact the service again.

## D033 — Physiotherapy sessions
Topic: Health

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a physiotherapy booking. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進物理治療預約。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係每次治療報價一百一十澳元。我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that each session is quoted at $110. My concern is that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That helps. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 咁就清楚喇。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我喺下星期五之前處理唔到，實際會有咩後果？
Model: What are the consequences if I can’t resolve this by next Friday?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有轉介信同保險資料。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will referral and insurer details be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?
Model: 相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要文件我可以畀你睇，仲有一份紀錄未搵到。
Model: I can show you the main documents; one record is missing.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the clinic patient portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經診所病人網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我點樣先知佢哋真係收到啲文件，我想有個編號，之後跟進都有嘢可以講。
Model: How can I tell they’ve actually received the documents, I want something I can refer to if I need to follow it up.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Right, I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?
Model: 明白，我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白，先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。
Model: Right, Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住差額自付費。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember gap payment. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks, that clears it up. I’ll keep the confirmation in case I need to come back to it.
Model: 唔該，咁就清楚喇。我會留返確認紀錄，之後要跟進都有得查。

## D034 — Mental health care plan
Topic: Health

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain a counselling referral. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋心理輔導轉介。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到家庭醫生預約係下星期三，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that the GP appointment is next Wednesday, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Got it. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺今個月底之前處理。否則，我擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
Model: I need to deal with it before the end of this month. Otherwise, I’m worried that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: One quick check: nothing has changed since that notice?
Model: 我快快問多句：嗰份通知之後冇再改過？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 啱，我呢邊而家都仲係嗰個金額。
Model: Correct; that’s the current amount on my side.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Sounds good. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有Medicare 卡同轉介信，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have Medicare card and referral letters, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?
Model: 相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分都有副本，仲欠一份資料。
Model: I have copies of most of them; one is still outstanding.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the clinic patient portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經診所病人網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 佢哋核對緊嗰陣，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？
Model: While they’re checking it, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about seven to ten business days. Meanwhile, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen.
Model: 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約七至十個工作日。期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果收到書面決定之後我都仲唔滿意，我要點樣跟正式程序提出覆核？
Model: If I’m still unhappy after I get the written decision, how do I challenge it through the right process?

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將心理健康治療計劃同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of mental health treatment plan with the reference number for future contact.

## D035 — Hospital discharge follow-up
Topic: Health

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of hospital discharge instructions. All right, I’ve got the matter on screen. What do you need help with first?
Model: 我哋睇下出院跟進指示嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，好，我而家睇到件事嘅資料。你想我先幫你處理邊一部分？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話跟進預約係七日後。我仍然擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。
Model: Earlier I was told the follow-up appointment is in seven days. I’m still concerned that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: This comes down to discharge summary. Remember that Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係出院摘要。要留意Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 有冇其他資料想我而家交，而唔係等過咗通知所列日期先補？
Model: Do you need anything else from me now, rather than after the date shown on the notice?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗出院摘要同藥物清單。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have discharge summary and medication list. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the clinic patient portal, then keep the submission receipt. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用診所病人網上平台提交，之後保存確認編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我做完整個申請之後，我應該等幾耐先再打電話？
Model: When I’ve finished the application, how long should I leave it before I call?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about two weeks. During that period, follow existing clinical advice and seek urgent help if symptoms worsen, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約兩星期。期間，照現有醫療建議做，如果症狀惡化就盡快求助，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果佢哋未處理完之前我搬屋或者換電話，我係咪更新返而家個申請，定係等有決定先？
Model: If I change address or phone number before they finish, do I update the current request or wait for the decision first?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低出院摘要，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down discharge summary so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D036 — Antenatal appointment
Topic: Health

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a clinic administration officer. I’m calling about an antenatal clinic booking. I can see the notes from your enquiry. What’s the part you’re most concerned about?
Model: 早晨。呢度係診所行政職員。我聯絡你係關於產前診所預約。我睇到你之前個查詢紀錄。你而家最擔心係邊一部分呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我最擔心治療可能延誤，或者我要支付預料之外嘅費用。我想喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理好。
Model: My concern is that treatment may be delayed or I may face an unexpected cost. I’d like this resolved before the next scheduled appointment or payment.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is antenatal care. Medicare arrangements and fees can vary by service and provider. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 而家要睇嘅重點係產前護理。Medicare 安排同收費會因服務同醫療提供者而有分別。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住第一次醫院預約係九月二十二日。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the first hospital appointment is on 22 September. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, ask the provider to confirm the booking, referral or fee in writing. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係要求醫療提供者以書面確認預約、轉介或者收費。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗轉介信、Medicare 卡同超聲波報告。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have referral, Medicare card and scan reports. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the clinic patient portal. Upload referral, Medicare card and scan reports, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用診所病人網上平台。上載轉介信、Medicare 卡同超聲波報告，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白，如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？
Model: Right, If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Right, Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.
Model: 明白，重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我睇完都覺得個決定唔啱，係咪有正式嘅覆核程序可以跟？
Model: If I still think the decision is wrong, is there a formal review process I can use?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask the practice manager or relevant health service to review the administrative issue.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，要求診所經理或者相關醫療服務覆核行政問題。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，我會咁樣做，亦會留返提交日期嘅證明。
Model: Fine, I’ll do it that way and keep proof of the date I sent it.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks. I’ll make sure I keep the receipt and a copy of the documents.
Model: 唔該。我會保存收據，同埋留返啲文件副本。

## D037 — Checking VEVO
Topic: Immigration and settlement

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a migration service officer. I can see an enquiry about a visa-status check. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟移民服務職員。我見到有一個關於簽證狀況查核嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到網上簽證權益查核系統，即係 VEVO。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions Visa Entitlement Verification Online, or VEVO, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record. Your current record also says the passport was renewed last month.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。你目前紀錄亦顯示上個月換咗新護照。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下星期五之前知道，因為可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before next Friday, because I may misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, check the current official notice and update the relevant details online.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗護照同簽證批准通知。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered passport and visa grant notice. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Are you able to show me those records today?
Model: 你今日方唔方便畀我睇嗰啲紀錄？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 有，淨係仲有一份文件要遲啲先拎到。
Model: Yes. There’s just one document I need to bring later.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use your ImmiAccount unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用你嘅 ImmiAccount。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我今日搞掂晒啲文件，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
Model: If I get the paperwork in today, what happens while the matter is being checked?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about five business days. While you wait, do not assume new work or travel rights until the current conditions are checked, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約五個工作日。等候期間，未核實現有條件之前，唔好自行假設有新嘅工作或者旅行權利，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果最後個決定對我不利，佢哋會唔會講明係申請邊一部分出問題？
Model: If the final answer goes against me, will they tell me what part of the application caused the problem?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返網上簽證權益查核系統，即係 VEVO呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use Visa Entitlement Verification Online, or VEVO when I contact the service again.

## D038 — Bridging Visa A
Topic: Immigration and settlement

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a bridging-visa status query. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進過橋簽證狀況查詢。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係現有實質簽證喺九月三十日到期。我擔心可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: This comes down to Bridging Visa A. In this type of matter, visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.
Model: 決定下一步之前，要記住A 類過橋簽證呢個詞。喺呢類事情，簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。

Source: 如果我喺今個月底之前處理唔到，實際會有咩後果？
Model: What are the consequences if I can’t resolve this by the end of this month?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, check the current official notice and update the relevant details online. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有簽證批准通知同護照。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Do you have most of that paperwork available?
Model: 你而家手上有冇大部分嗰啲文件？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 差唔多齊，我今晚可以攞埋欠嗰份。
Model: Nearly all. I can get the missing one this evening.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through your ImmiAccount. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經你嘅 ImmiAccount提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我上載完會唔會有嘢證明成功咗，如果之後有爭議，嗰個日期可能好重要㗎。
Model: Will I get anything showing the upload went through, That date could matter if there’s a dispute later.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Sure, If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?
Model: 可以，如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 可以，重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。
Model: Sure, Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住A 類過橋簽證。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Bridging Visa A. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.
Model: 好，好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。

## D039 — Bridging Visa B travel
Topic: Immigration and settlement

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain proposed overseas travel. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋計劃出境旅行。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到計劃十月份出境十日，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that travel is planned for ten days in October, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: All right. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺通知所列日期之前處理。否則，我擔心可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。
Model: I need to deal with it before the date shown on the notice. Otherwise, What worries me is that I could misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Just checking: that’s still the latest version you received?
Model: 確認一下，呢個係你最近收到嗰個版本？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 啱，我今朝都再對過一次。
Model: Correct. I checked it again this morning.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That’s clear. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有旅行日期、護照同簽證通知，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have travel dates, passport and visa notice, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Have you brought the records we just discussed?
Model: 頭先講嗰啲紀錄你有冇帶嚟？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 差唔多全部有，不過仲有一樣冇帶喺身。
Model: I’ve got nearly everything, but one item isn’t with me.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through your ImmiAccount and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經你嘅 ImmiAccount上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 佢哋核對緊嗰陣，會唔會有額外收費或者其他後果㗎？
Model: While they’re checking it, could I be charged anything extra or face another consequence?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, do not assume new work or travel rights until the current conditions are checked.
Model: 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約兩星期。期間，未核實現有條件之前，唔好自行假設有新嘅工作或者旅行權利。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果收到書面決定之後我都仲唔滿意，我下一步應該搵邊個，先唔使又打同一輪電話？
Model: If I’m still unhappy after I get the written decision, who should I contact next instead of making the same phone call again?

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將B 類過橋簽證同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of Bridging Visa B with the reference number for future contact.

## D040 — Visa condition enquiry
Topic: Immigration and settlement

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of current visa conditions. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.
Model: 我哋睇下現有簽證條件嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話新更表每星期會多十二個工作鐘。我仍然擔心可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。
Model: Earlier I was told a new roster would add twelve work hours a week. I’m still concerned that I may misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I can see that note. The key term on this file is visa condition. Remember that visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係簽證條件。要留意簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 有冇其他資料想我而家交，而唔係等過咗下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期先補？
Model: Do you need anything else from me now, rather than after the next scheduled appointment or payment?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to check the current official notice and update the relevant details online. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗簽證批准通知同建議更表。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have visa grant notice and proposed roster. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use your ImmiAccount, then keep the submission receipt. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用你嘅 ImmiAccount提交，之後保存網上確認資料。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 啲文件交齊之後，大概幾時再問吓會比較合理？
Model: Once the documents are in, what’s a sensible time to ask for an update?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about three to four weeks. During that period, do not assume new work or travel rights until the current conditions are checked, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約三至四星期。期間，未核實現有條件之前，唔好自行假設有新嘅工作或者旅行權利，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果件事完成之前有重要變更，我係咪應該即刻通知佢哋？
Model: If there’s a material change before the matter is finalised, should I tell them straight away?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低簽證條件，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down visa condition so I can refer to the matter accurately.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: If I receive another letter about the same matter, should I bring both copies when I follow up?
Model: 如果同一件事我又收到另一封信，之後跟進嗰陣係咪兩封都要帶？

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係，兩封都留住。上面嘅日期同參考編號可以幫職員分清邊份通知係最新。
Model: Yes, keep both. The dates and reference numbers can help staff work out which notice is the most recent.

## D041 — Updating passport details
Topic: Immigration and settlement

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a migration service officer. I’m calling about new passport details. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.
Model: 早晨。呢度係移民服務職員。我聯絡你係關於新護照資料。多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有啲驚會誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。我想喺下星期五之前處理好。
Model: I’m concerned I might misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions. I’d like this resolved before next Friday.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The part that matters here is ImmiAccount update. visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
Model: 而家要睇嘅重點係ImmiAccount 資料更新。簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住新護照喺八月二日簽發。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the new passport was issued on 2 August. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, check the current official notice and update the relevant details online. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗新護照個人資料頁。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have new passport biodata page. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use your ImmiAccount. Upload new passport biodata page, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用你嘅 ImmiAccount。上載新護照個人資料頁，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 可以，我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？
Model: Sure, I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Sure, Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.
Model: 可以，先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我哋預計大約四十人去個會堂。如果人數再多啲，係咪要更新個預約？
Model: We expect about forty people at the hall. Do I need to update the booking if the number increases?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, tell the venue before the event because capacity, insurance or supervision requirements may change with attendance numbers.
Model: 係，活動之前通知場地，因為人數改變可能會影響場地容量、保險或者監督安排。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白。我會留返確認紀錄，有需要先再跟進。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the confirmation and follow up only if I need to.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thank you. I’ll check the details once more before I submit anything.
Model: 唔該。我提交之前會再對一次啲資料。

## D042 — Citizenship appointment
Topic: Immigration and settlement

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a migration service officer. I can see an enquiry about a citizenship appointment. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟移民服務職員。我見到有一個關於入籍預約嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到身份證明文件。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions identity documents, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because visa status and conditions should be checked against the current electronic record. Your current record also says the appointment is at 9:30 am on 15 October.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為簽證狀況同條件應以目前電子紀錄為準。你目前紀錄亦顯示預約係十月十五日上午九點半。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺今個月底之前知道，因為可能誤解現有簽證權利或者條件。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the end of this month, because I may misunderstand my current visa rights or conditions.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, check the current official notice and update the relevant details online.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要查看最新正式通知，並喺網上更新相關資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗護照、有相身份證同預約信。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered passport, photo ID and appointment letter. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?
Model: 相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要紀錄有齊，仲有一份文件要下載返。
Model: I have the main records. One document still needs to be downloaded.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use your ImmiAccount unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用你嘅 ImmiAccount。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假設我今日下晝上載晒，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
Model: Say I upload all of this this afternoon, what happens while the matter is being checked?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about seven to ten business days. While you wait, do not assume new work or travel rights until the current conditions are checked, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約七至十個工作日。等候期間，未核實現有條件之前，唔好自行假設有新嘅工作或者旅行權利，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果佢哋最後話唔得，我可唔可以睇咗理由先，再決定使唔使申請覆核？
Model: If they eventually say no, can I see the reasons before deciding whether to seek a review?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, follow the contact or review pathway stated in the official notice.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，按正式通知所列嘅聯絡或者覆核途徑處理。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返身份證明文件呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use identity documents when I contact the service again.

## D043 — AMEP English classes
Topic: Immigration and settlement

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on English-class options after settlement. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進定居後英文課程選擇。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係星期二同星期四夜晚有時間。我擔心可能錯過註冊程序或者合適課程選擇。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that Tuesday and Thursday evenings are available. I’m concerned I might miss an enrolment step or a suitable class option.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: No problem. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 冇問題。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 去到通知所列日期件事仲未解決，我應該預咩情況？
Model: If the date shown on the notice arrives and this is still unresolved, what should I expect?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, ask for an AMEP eligibility assessment and suitable class options. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，查詢 AMEP 資格評估同合適上課選擇。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有資格資料同有相身份證。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will eligibility details and photo ID be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Are those documents available on your phone now?
Model: 嗰啲文件而家用電話開唔開到？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分有呀，不過仲有一份喺屋企。
Model: I have most of them; one is still at home.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the service enrolment portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經服務註冊網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我點樣先知佢哋真係收到啲文件，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。
Model: How can I tell they’ve actually received the documents, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Note the lodgement reference. Allow about two weeks; during that time, keep your identity and eligibility documents ready for enrolment.
Model: 保存確認編號。預大約兩星期；期間，準備好身份同資格文件，方便註冊。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如到時我仲係等緊，點樣跟進先唔會變咗重複申請？
Model: Suppose I’m still waiting after that, what’s the right way to chase it up without duplicating the request?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask the provider or settlement service to explain the available enrolment options.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，要求課程提供者或者定居服務解釋可用註冊選擇。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住成人移民英語計劃，即係 AMEP。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Adult Migrant English Program, or AMEP. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks. I’ll make sure I keep the receipt and a copy of the documents.
Model: 唔該。我會保存收據，同埋留返啲文件副本。

## D044 — Qualification recognition
Topic: Immigration and settlement

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain an overseas qualification enquiry. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋海外學歷查詢。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到二零二二年喺香港完成學位，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that the degree was completed in Hong Kong in 2022, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Fair enough. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺我提交下一份工作或者升學申請之前處理。否則，我擔心可能用錯工作或者升學所需嘅學歷認可途徑。
Model: I need to deal with it before I submit my next employment or study application. Otherwise, I’m anxious that I may follow the wrong recognition pathway for work or study.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: So there isn’t a newer figure I should be looking at?
Model: 即係冇另一個更新嘅數字要睇？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 啱呀，我而家都仲係按呢個數字。
Model: Yes, I’m still working from that figure.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 唔該，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有學位證書同成績表，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have a degree certificate and academic transcript, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Are those supporting papers with you now?
Model: 啲證明文件而家有冇喺身？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分有呀，不過仲有一份喺屋企。
Model: I have most of them; one is still at home.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the relevant assessing body’s online portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經相關評估機構嘅網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 等佢哋作決定之前，會唔會有額外收費或者其他後果㗎？
Model: Until they make a decision, could I be charged anything extra or face another consequence?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, keep original qualification documents and any required translations available.
Model: 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約三至四星期。期間，準備好學歷文件正本同任何所需翻譯。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果收到書面決定之後我都仲唔滿意，我下一步應該搵邊個，先唔使又打同一輪電話？
Model: If I’m still unhappy after I get the written decision, who should I contact next instead of making the same phone call again?

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask the assessing body to explain its assessment or review process.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，要求評估機構解釋評估或者覆核程序。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將學歷評估同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of qualification assessment with the reference number for future contact.

## D045 — Traffic fine court option
Topic: Legal

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of a traffic infringement notice. I’ve found the enquiry. Before we get into the details, tell me what you need resolved.
Model: 我哋睇下交通違規罰款通知嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我搵到你個查詢喇。講細節之前，你最想解決嘅係咩呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話通知要求九月九日前處理。我仍然擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。
Model: Earlier I was told the notice requires action by 9 September. I’m still concerned that I may miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: For this matter, we need to be clear about infringement notice. Remember that deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. Let me check how that applies to you.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係違規罰款通知。要留意除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我呢邊而家係咪齊晒？我唔想過咗下星期五先知有嘢漏咗。
Model: Is my side complete now? I don’t want to discover something is missing after next Friday.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗通知、駕駛執照資料同相片。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have notice, licence details and photographs. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the court or service online portal, then note the lodgement reference. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用法院或者服務機構網上平台提交，之後留返提交收據。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 上載完成之後，大概幾時再問吓會比較合理？
Model: After the upload is complete, what’s a sensible time to ask for an update?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Understood. I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?
Model: 明白。我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白。先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。
Model: Understood. Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低違規罰款通知，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down infringement notice so I can refer to the matter accurately.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can I write down the reference now? I do not want to lose it when I call back.
Model: 我可唔可以而家抄低個參考編號？我唔想下次再打嚟嗰陣搵唔返。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 當然可以。放埋喺你啲筆記度，以後就呢件事聯絡服務機構時都報返個編號。
Model: Of course. Keep it with your notes, and quote it whenever you contact the service about this matter.

## D046 — NSW AVO enquiry
Topic: Legal

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a community legal service officer. I’m calling about an AVO matter in New South Wales. I can see the notes from your enquiry. What’s the part you’re most concerned about?
Model: 早晨。呢度係社區法律服務職員。我聯絡你係關於新州 AVO 個案。我睇到你之前個查詢紀錄。你而家最擔心係邊一部分呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我就係怕可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。我想喺今個月底之前處理好。
Model: I’m nervous that I might miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction. I’d like this resolved before the end of this month.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is Apprehended Violence Order, or AVO. deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 呢個情況主要關乎暴力禁制令，即係 AVO。除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。不如我哋先核對你而家嘅資料，再決定下一步。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住下一次地方法院日期係九月二十一日。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the next Local Court date is 21 September. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗法院文件同臨時命令。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have court papers and interim order. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the court or service online portal. Upload court papers and interim order, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用法院或者服務機構網上平台。上載法院文件同臨時命令，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
Model: How long should I wait? I’m concerned I might miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about seven to ten business days. Meanwhile, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
Model: 預大約七至十個工作日。期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 封信已經有聆訊日期。如果我嗰朝去唔到，日期之前應該聯絡邊個？
Model: The hearing date is already in my letter. If I cannot attend that morning, who do I contact before the date?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Contact the court or tribunal listed on the notice as early as possible. Do not assume the hearing is changed until you receive confirmation.
Model: 盡早聯絡通知上面寫明嘅法院或者審裁處。未收到確認之前，唔好當聆訊日期已經更改。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 唔該晒。我會照住啲步驟做，之後收到新通知亦會睇清楚。
Model: Thanks for your help. I’ll follow those steps and check the next notice carefully.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Understood. I’ll keep the confirmation and follow up only if I need to.
Model: 明白。我會留返確認紀錄，有需要先再跟進。

## D047 — Bail reporting condition
Topic: Legal

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a community legal service officer. I can see an enquiry about bail reporting conditions. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟社區法律服務職員。我見到有一個關於保釋報到條件嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到保釋條件。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions bail condition, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. Your current record also says reporting is required every Monday before 6 pm.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。你目前紀錄亦顯示逢星期一下午六點前要報到。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺通知所列日期之前知道，因為可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the date shown on the notice, because I may miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗保釋文件同工作更表。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered bail undertaking and work roster. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you pull up those documents on your phone?
Model: 你可唔可以而家用電話開返啲文件？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分帶咗，不過有一頁留咗喺屋企。
Model: I brought most of it, but one page is at home.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the court or service online portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用法院或者服務機構網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假設我今日下晝上載晒，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
Model: Say I upload all of this this afternoon, what happens while the matter is being checked?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about two weeks. While you wait, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約兩星期。等候期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我收到一個負面決定，我可唔可以叫佢哋講清楚點解，再決定係咪覆核？
Model: If I get a negative decision, can I ask them to explain exactly why before I challenge it?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返保釋條件呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use bail condition when I contact the service again.

## D048 — Witness statement
Topic: Legal

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a witness-statement appointment. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進錄取證人供詞預約。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係會面安排喺星期四下晝。我擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that the interview is booked for Thursday afternoon. I’m concerned I might miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is witness statement. In this type of matter, deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 決定下一步之前，要記住證人供詞呢個詞。喺呢類事情，除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 去到下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期件事仲未解決，我應該預咩情況？
Model: If the next scheduled appointment or payment arrives and this is still unresolved, what should I expect?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有預約信同相關訊息。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will appointment letter and relevant messages be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you access the evidence we just mentioned?
Model: 頭先提過嗰啲證明你而家開唔開到？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要文件我可以畀你睇，仲有一份紀錄未搵到。
Model: I can show you the main documents; one record is missing.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the court or service online portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經法院或者服務機構網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我上載完會唔會有嘢證明成功咗，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。
Model: Will I get anything showing the upload went through, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Save the online confirmation. Allow about three to four weeks; during that time, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction.
Model: 記低提交參考編號。預大約三至四星期；期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如到時我仲係等緊，我應該跟返原本個申請，定係再交一份？
Model: Suppose I’m still waiting after that, should I follow up the existing request or submit another one?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住證人供詞。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember witness statement. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Perfect. I’ll sort it out and keep the confirmation for later.
Model: 好極喇。我會處理好，亦會留返確認紀錄。

## D049 — Family dispute mediation
Topic: Legal

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain a family dispute-resolution appointment. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋家庭爭議解決預約。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到調解安排喺十月六日，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺下星期五之前處理。否則，我擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。
