**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I want to make sure that date is the current one.
Model: 我想確定嗰個日期係而家最新嗰個。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我收到最新嘅紀錄就係呢份。
Model: That’s the newest record I’ve received.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有預約信同育兒安排建議，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have appointment letter and parenting proposals, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Did you bring the paperwork we talked about?
Model: 頭先講嗰啲文件你有冇帶埋嚟？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要紀錄有齊，仲有一份文件要下載返。
Model: I have the main records. One document still needs to be downloaded.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the court or service online portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經法院或者服務機構網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 佢哋未核對完份紀錄之前，會唔會有額外收費或者其他後果㗎？
Model: Before they finish reviewing the record, could I be charged anything extra or face another consequence?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about five business days. Meanwhile, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction.
Model: 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約五個工作日。期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我睇完書面決定都仲係唔明，正確下一步係咩？我唔想不停打去唔同地方。
Model: If the written decision still doesn’t make sense, what’s the proper next step? I don’t want to keep ringing different people.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將家庭爭議解決同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of family dispute resolution with the reference number for future contact.

## D050 — Small civil claim
Topic: Legal

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of a small civil claim. I can see the notes from your enquiry. What’s the part you’re most concerned about?
Model: 我哋睇下小額民事索償嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我睇到你之前個查詢紀錄。你而家最擔心係邊一部分呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話爭議金額係三千二百澳元。我仍然擔心可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。
Model: Earlier I was told the amount in dispute is $3,200. I’m still concerned that I may miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I can see that note. The key term on this file is civil claim. Remember that deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係民事索償。要留意除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 今個月底之前可唔可以再幫我對一次份紀錄，睇吓有冇漏？
Model: Could you check the file once more before the end of this month, just in case I’ve left something out?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗銀行轉賬紀錄同書面訊息。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have bank transfers and written messages. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the court or service online portal, then save the confirmation number. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用法院或者服務機構網上平台提交，之後保存網上確認資料。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我交咗之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？
Model: After I submit it, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Understood. If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?
Model: 明白。如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白。重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。
Model: Understood. Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低民事索償，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down civil claim so I can refer to the matter accurately.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can I write down the reference now? I do not want to lose it when I call back.
Model: 我可唔可以而家抄低個參考編號？我唔想下次再打嚟嗰陣搵唔返。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 當然可以。放埋喺你啲筆記度，以後就呢件事聯絡服務機構時都報返個編號。
Model: Of course. Keep it with your notes, and quote it whenever you contact the service about this matter.

## D051 — Legal aid eligibility
Topic: Legal

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a community legal service officer. I’m calling about a legal-aid enquiry. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
Model: 早晨。呢度係社區法律服務職員。我聯絡你係關於法律援助查詢。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我就係怕可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。我想喺通知所列日期之前處理好。
Model: What worries me is that I may miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction. I’d like this resolved before the date shown on the notice.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The part that matters here is legal aid. deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
Model: 而家要睇嘅重點係法律援助。除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。我哋按你實際情況逐樣睇，唔好估住先。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住三個星期後要上庭。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the court date is in three weeks. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗收入證明同法院文件。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have income evidence and court documents. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the court or service online portal. Upload income evidence and court documents, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用法院或者服務機構網上平台。上載收入證明同法院文件，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？
Model: Good, I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good, Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.
Model: 好，先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如我覺得個決定有問題，我可唔可以先要求覆核，再決定使唔使投訴呀？
Model: Suppose the decision doesn’t seem right, can I ask for a review before making a complaint?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 唔該。我提交之前會再對一次啲資料。
Model: Thank you. I’ll check the details once more before I submit anything.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: All right, I know what to do now. I’ll keep a copy of whatever I send.
Model: 好，而家我知點做喇。我交出去嘅資料都會留返副本。

## D052 — Requesting an adjournment
Topic: Legal

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a community legal service officer. I can see an enquiry about an upcoming court hearing. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟社區法律服務職員。我見到有一個關於即將進行嘅法院聆訊嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到押後聆訊。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions adjournment, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because deadlines and formal directions must be followed unless they are officially changed. Your current record also says the hearing is listed for 3 September.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為除非正式更改，否則限期同正式指示都要遵守。你目前紀錄亦顯示聆訊排期係九月三日。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前知道，因為可能錯過限期，或者無意中違反正式指示。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the next scheduled appointment or payment, because I may miss a deadline or accidentally breach a formal direction.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, get advice promptly and respond through the correct legal process.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要盡快取得意見，並用正確法律程序回應。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗法院通知同醫療證明。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered court notice and medical evidence. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, Can you pull up those documents on your phone?
Model: 好，你可唔可以而家用電話開返啲文件？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要嗰幾份有，一個檔案仲喺屋企部電腦。
Model: I have the main ones. One file is on my home computer.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the court or service online portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用法院或者服務機構網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我今日交齊啲資料，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
Model: If I send everything today, what happens while the matter is being checked?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about three to four weeks. While you wait, keep every notice and do not ignore any court or police direction, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約三至四星期。等候期間，保留所有通知，亦唔好忽視任何法院或者警方指示，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果個申請畀人拒絕，我可唔可以叫佢哋講清楚點解，再決定係咪覆核？
Model: If the application is refused, can I ask them to explain exactly why before I challenge it?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, get legal advice about the available review, legal-aid or court step.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，就可用嘅覆核、法律援助或者法院程序取得法律意見。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返押後聆訊呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use adjournment when I contact the service again.

## D053 — Hard-waste collection
Topic: Community

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a council hard-waste booking. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進市議會大型廢物收集預約。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係預約收集日期係九月十四日。我擔心可能錯過預約或者用唔到服務。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that the booking is for 14 September. What worries me is that I may miss the booking or lose access to the service.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is hard-waste collection. In this type of matter, local services usually depend on the correct address, eligibility and booking details. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 決定下一步之前，要記住大型廢物收集呢個詞。喺呢類事情，本地服務通常視乎正確地址、資格同預約資料。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我喺下星期五之前處理唔到，實際會有咩後果？
Model: What are the consequences if I can’t resolve this by next Friday?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, submit the council booking or application with the requested information. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，連同所需資料提交市議會預約或者申請。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有預約確認同物業地址。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will booking confirmation and property address be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Do you have the supporting documents with you today?
Model: 你今日有冇帶埋啲證明文件？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 差唔多齊晒，我返屋企可以上載最後一份。
Model: Almost everything. I can upload the last item from home.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the council online services portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經市議會網上服務平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我正式提交之後，點知系統已經有紀錄，如果之後有爭議，嗰個日期可能好重要㗎。
Model: Once I lodge it, how will I know it’s on their system, That date could matter if there’s a dispute later.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Certainly. I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?
Model: 可以。我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 可以。先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。
Model: Certainly. Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, use the council complaint or written-review process.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，使用市議會投訴或者書面覆核程序。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住大型廢物收集。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember hard-waste collection. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks. I’ll make sure I keep the receipt and a copy of the documents.
Model: 唔該。我會保存收據，同埋留返啲文件副本。

## D054 — Library membership
Topic: Community

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain public-library membership. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋公共圖書館會籍。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到今個星期六想借書，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。

Source: Right, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: One quick check: nothing has changed since that notice?
Model: 我快快問多句：嗰份通知之後冇再改過？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 呢個而家仲係最新資料。
Model: That’s still the current information.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Sure, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有有相身份證同電子地址證明，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have photo ID and digital proof of address, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Have you got the main documents with you?
Model: 主要嗰幾份文件你有冇帶喺身？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要文件準備好喇，遲啲我會再補多一份。
Model: The main documents are ready. I’ll add one more later.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the council online services portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經市議會網上服務平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 佢哋未核對完份紀錄之前，有冇啲費用或者實際影響我應該知道？
Model: Before they finish reviewing the record, is there a cost or practical consequence I should know about?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good, If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?
Model: 好，如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。
Model: Good, Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then use the council complaint or written-review process.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，使用市議會投訴或者書面覆核程序。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將圖書證同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of library card with the reference number for future contact.

## D055 — Community-centre course
Topic: Community

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of a community-centre course booking. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.
Model: 我哋睇下社區中心課程預約嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話八星期課程收費九十六澳元。我仍然擔心可能錯過預約或者用唔到服務。
Model: Earlier I was told the eight-week course costs $96. I’m still concerned that I may miss the booking or lose access to the service.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: For this matter, we need to be clear about concession fee. Remember that local services usually depend on the correct address, eligibility and booking details. Let me check how that applies to you.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係優惠收費。要留意本地服務通常視乎正確地址、資格同預約資料。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我呢邊而家係咪齊晒？我唔想過咗通知所列日期先知有嘢漏咗。
Model: Is my side complete now? I don’t want to discover something is missing after the date shown on the notice.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to submit the council booking or application with the requested information. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係連同所需資料提交市議會預約或者申請。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗預約電郵同優惠資格證明。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have booking email and concession evidence. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the council online services portal, then keep the electronic acknowledgement. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用市議會網上服務平台提交，之後留返提交收據。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我交咗之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？
Model: After I submit it, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about two weeks. During that period, keep your booking or request number, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約兩星期。期間，保留預約或者申請編號，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如評估期間我有一項資料改咗，點樣更新會最穩陣？
Model: Suppose one of my details changes during the assessment, what’s the safest way to update it?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the council complaint or written-review process.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，使用市議會投訴或者書面覆核程序。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低優惠收費，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down concession fee so I can refer to the matter accurately.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: If I receive another letter about the same matter, should I bring both copies when I follow up?
Model: 如果同一件事我又收到另一封信，之後跟進嗰陣係咪兩封都要帶？

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係，兩封都留住。上面嘅日期同參考編號可以幫職員分清邊份通知係最新。
Model: Yes, keep both. The dates and reference numbers can help staff work out which notice is the most recent.

## D056 — Resident parking permit
Topic: Community

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a local council customer service officer. I’m calling about a resident parking permit. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
Model: 早晨。呢度係市議會客戶服務職員。我聯絡你係關於居民泊車許可證。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 而家我最憂心係可能錯過預約或者用唔到服務。我想喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理好。
Model: My main worry is that I could miss the booking or lose access to the service. I’d like this resolved before the next scheduled appointment or payment.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The part that matters here is resident parking permit. local services usually depend on the correct address, eligibility and booking details. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
Model: 我哋而家要睇居民泊車許可證。本地服務通常視乎正確地址、資格同預約資料。我即刻幫你對一對件紀錄。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住舊許可證喺八月三十一日到期。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the old permit expires on 31 August. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, submit the council booking or application with the requested information. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係連同所需資料提交市議會預約或者申請。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗駕駛執照、租約同車輛登記資料。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have licence, lease and vehicle registration. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the council online services portal. Upload licence, lease and vehicle registration, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用市議會網上服務平台。上載駕駛執照、租約同車輛登記資料，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 可以。如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？
Model: Certainly. If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Certainly. Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.
Model: 可以。重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我懷疑個機構搞錯咗，我應該搵邊個再睇一次個決定？
Model: If I believe the service has made a mistake, who do I ask to look at the decision again?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, use the council complaint or written-review process.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，使用市議會投訴或者書面覆核程序。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 咁就明喇。我會補返欠嗰份資料，再留低個參考編號。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll sort out the missing item and keep the reference number.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Perfect. I’ll sort it out and keep the confirmation for later.
Model: 好極喇。我會處理好，亦會留返確認紀錄。

## D057 — Pet registration
Topic: Community

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a local council customer service officer. I can see an enquiry about a dog registration. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟市議會客戶服務職員。我見到有一個關於狗隻登記嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到寵物登記。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions pet registration, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because local services usually depend on the correct address, eligibility and booking details. Your current record also says the dog is microchipped and three years old.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為本地服務通常視乎正確地址、資格同預約資料。你目前紀錄亦顯示隻狗有晶片，而家三歲。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下星期五之前知道，因為可能錯過預約或者用唔到服務。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before next Friday, because I may miss the booking or lose access to the service.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, submit the council booking or application with the requested information.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要連同所需資料提交市議會預約或者申請。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗晶片資料同地址證明。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered microchip details and proof of address. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you access those records right now?
Model: 你而家開唔開到嗰啲紀錄？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要文件我可以畀你睇，仲有一份紀錄未搵到。
Model: I can show you the main documents; one record is missing.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the council online services portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用市議會網上服務平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假設我今日下晝上載晒，佢哋評估嗰陣咁我應該點做呀？我唔想又整多個問題出嚟。
Model: Say I upload all of this this afternoon, what should I do while they assess it? I don’t want to create another problem.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about five business days. While you wait, keep your booking or request number, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約五個工作日。等候期間，保留預約或者申請編號，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果個申請畀人拒絕，我可唔可以叫佢哋講清楚點解，再決定係咪覆核？
Model: If the application is refused, can I ask them to explain exactly why before I challenge it?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, use the council complaint or written-review process.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，使用市議會投訴或者書面覆核程序。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返寵物登記呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use pet registration when I contact the service again.

## D058 — Volunteering with children
Topic: Community

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a volunteer application. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進義工申請。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係個計劃喺九月十九日開始。我擔心可能錯過預約或者用唔到服務。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that the program begins on 19 September. The thing I’m worried about is that I may miss the booking or lose access to the service.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Understood. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我可能趕唔切今個月底之前搞掂，咁會唔會有問題？
Model: I may not get this done by the end of this month. Will that cause a problem?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, apply for the required screening check using the correct jurisdictional process. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，用所在司法管轄區嘅正確程序申請所需背景審查。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有身份證明同義工資料。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will identity documents and volunteer details be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Have you got those records ready to send?
Model: 嗰啲紀錄你而家準備好可以交未？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要嗰幾份有，一個檔案仲喺屋企部電腦。
Model: I have the main ones. One file is on my home computer.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the relevant state or territory screening portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經相關州或者領地嘅背景審查網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我點樣證明自己係邊日交文件，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。
Model: How do I prove what day I sent the documents, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Save the online confirmation. Allow about seven to ten business days; during that time, do not start restricted child-related duties until the organisation confirms the required clearance.
Model: 保存確認編號。預大約七至十個工作日；期間，機構確認所需審查完成之前，唔好開始受限制嘅兒童相關工作。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果過咗嗰段時間都冇變化，我係咪應該問返而家嗰份申請，定係重新開始？
Model: What if that timeframe passes and nothing changes, do I call about the current application, or start again?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask the screening authority or volunteer organisation to explain the application status.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，要求審查機構或者義工機構解釋申請狀況。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住兒童工作審查，即係 WWCC。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Working with Children Check, or WWCC. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

## D059 — Neighbourhood noise complaint
Topic: Community

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain a neighbourhood noise complaint. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋鄰里噪音投訴。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到滋擾通常夜晚十一點後開始，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that the disturbance usually starts after 11 pm, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺通知所列日期之前處理。否則，我擔心可能錯過預約或者用唔到服務。
Model: I need to deal with it before the date shown on the notice. Otherwise, I’m concerned I might miss the booking or lose access to the service.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Is the amount you mentioned still accurate?
Model: 你講嗰個金額而家仲準確？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係，之後冇再收到另一封通知。
Model: Yes. There hasn’t been another notice.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Certainly. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 可以。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有日期、時間同日誌紀錄，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have dates, times and diary notes, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you show me the supporting records today?
Model: 你今日可唔可以畀我睇吓啲證明資料？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 差唔多齊，我今晚可以攞埋欠嗰份。
Model: Nearly all. I can get the missing one this evening.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the council online services portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經市議會網上服務平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 佢哋未核對完份紀錄之前，有冇啲費用或者實際影響我應該知道？
Model: Before they finish reviewing the record, is there a cost or practical consequence I should know about?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, keep your booking or request number.
Model: 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約兩星期。期間，保留預約或者申請編號。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果封決定信我睇完都覺得有錯，我要點樣跟正式程序提出覆核？
Model: If I read the decision and still think it’s wrong, how do I challenge it through the right process?

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then use the council complaint or written-review process.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，使用市議會投訴或者書面覆核程序。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將噪音投訴同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of noise complaint with the reference number for future contact.

## D060 — Sports-ground booking
Topic: Community

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of a community sports-ground booking. All right, I’ve got the matter on screen. What do you need help with first?
Model: 我哋睇下社區運動場預約嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，好，我而家睇到件事嘅資料。你想我先幫你處理邊一部分？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話活動係九月二十六日，大約四十人。我仍然擔心可能錯過預約或者用唔到服務。
Model: Earlier I was told the event is for forty people on 26 September. I’m still concerned that I may miss the booking or lose access to the service.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: This comes down to facility hire. Remember that local services usually depend on the correct address, eligibility and booking details. Let’s check your actual circumstances before we decide the next step.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係場地租用。要留意本地服務通常視乎正確地址、資格同預約資料。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 去到下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前，你可唔可以幫我睇吓仲欠唔欠資料？
Model: Before the next scheduled appointment or payment, can you check whether you still need anything from me?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to submit the council booking or application with the requested information. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係連同所需資料提交市議會預約或者申請。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗預約表同公眾責任保險資料。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have booking form and public-liability details. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the council online services portal, then keep the electronic acknowledgement. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用市議會網上服務平台提交，之後留返電子確認紀錄。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我做完整個申請之後，通常幾時先適合跟進呀？
Model: When I’ve finished the application, when would it be reasonable to follow up?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about three to four weeks. During that period, keep your booking or request number, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約三至四星期。期間，保留預約或者申請編號，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果件事完成之前有重要變更，我係咪更新返而家個申請，定係等有決定先？
Model: If there’s a material change before the matter is finalised, do I update the current request or wait for the decision first?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the council complaint or written-review process.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，使用市議會投訴或者書面覆核程序。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低場地租用，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down facility hire so I can refer to the matter accurately.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can I write down the reference now? I do not want to lose it when I call back.
Model: 我可唔可以而家抄低個參考編號？我唔想下次再打嚟嗰陣搵唔返。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 當然可以。放埋喺你啲筆記度，以後就呢件事聯絡服務機構時都報返個編號。
Model: Of course. Keep it with your notes, and quote it whenever you contact the service about this matter.

## D061 — Primary-school enrolment
Topic: Education

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a school administration officer. I’m calling about a child’s school enrolment. All right, I’ve got the matter on screen. What do you need help with first?
Model: 早晨。呢度係學校行政職員。我聯絡你係關於小朋友小學入學申請。好，我而家睇到件事嘅資料。你想我先幫你處理邊一部分？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 而家我最憂心係可能錯過註冊，或者要承擔本來可以避免嘅費用。我想喺下星期五之前處理好。
Model: The thing I’m worried about is that I may miss enrolment or become liable for an avoidable fee. I’d like this resolved before next Friday.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is local intake area. enrolment requirements can depend on the school, local intake arrangements and supporting documents. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 呢個情況主要關乎本地收生區。入學要求可能視乎學校、本地收生安排同證明文件。不如我哋先核對你而家嘅資料，再決定下一步。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住一家上星期搬到呢個區。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the family moved to the suburb last week. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, complete the enrolment enquiry and provide the requested address and student documents. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係完成入學查詢並提供所需地址同學生文件。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗地址證明同小朋友資料。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have proof of address and child records. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the school enrolment portal. Upload proof of address and child records, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用學校入學網上平台。上載地址證明同小朋友資料，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能錯過註冊，或者要承擔本來可以避免嘅費用。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
Model: How long should I wait? I’m anxious that I may miss enrolment or become liable for an avoidable fee. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about five business days. Meanwhile, keep the school informed if the address or contact details change. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
Model: 預大約五個工作日。期間，如果地址或者聯絡資料有變，要通知學校。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我睇完都覺得個決定唔啱，我可唔可以先要求覆核，再決定使唔使投訴呀？
Model: If I still think the decision is wrong, can I ask for a review before making a complaint?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask the school to explain the enrolment decision or the relevant education-department pathway.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，要求學校解釋入學決定或者相關教育部門途徑。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白，好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。
Model: Right, Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good, I’ll do that. If anything changes, I’ll let the service know straight away.
Model: 好呀，我會照做。如果有任何改動，我會即刻通知返個機構。

## D062 — Parent-teacher meeting
Topic: Education

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a school administration officer. I can see an enquiry about a parent-teacher interview. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟學校行政職員。我見到有一個關於家長教師面談嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到家長教師面談。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions parent-teacher interview, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because meeting and interpreter arrangements depend on the school booking and available support. Your current record also says the appointment is at 4:20 pm next Tuesday.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為面談同傳譯安排會視乎學校預約同可用支援。你目前紀錄亦顯示預約係下星期二下晝四點二十分。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺今個月底之前知道，因為可能錯過註冊，或者要承擔本來可以避免嘅費用。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the end of this month, because I may miss enrolment or become liable for an avoidable fee.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, confirm the meeting time and interpreter request with the school.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要同學校確認面談時間同傳譯員要求。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗預約確認同學生資料。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered booking confirmation and student details. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Do you have most of that paperwork available?
Model: 你而家手上有冇大部分嗰啲文件？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要紀錄有齊，仲有一份文件要下載返。
Model: I have the main records. One document still needs to be downloaded.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the school booking portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用學校預約網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我今日交齊啲資料，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
Model: If I send everything today, what happens while the matter is being checked?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about seven to ten business days. While you wait, keep the booking confirmation and tell the school promptly about any change, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約七至十個工作日。等候期間，保留預約確認，如有變更盡快通知學校，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果最後個決定對我不利，佢哋會唔會講明係申請邊一部分出問題？
Model: If the final answer goes against me, will they tell me what part of the application caused the problem?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, ask the school office to check the booking or support request.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，要求學校辦公室核對預約或者支援要求。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返家長教師面談呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use parent-teacher interview when I contact the service again.

## D063 — School absence
Topic: Education

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a recent school absence. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進最近學校缺課。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係因病缺課三日。我擔心可能錯過註冊，或者要承擔本來可以避免嘅費用。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that three days were missed because of illness. I’m nervous that I might miss enrolment or become liable for an avoidable fee.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 唔該。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 去到通知所列日期件事仲未解決，我應該預咩情況？
Model: If the date shown on the notice arrives and this is still unresolved, what should I expect?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, send the absence explanation and supporting evidence to the school. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，向學校提交缺席解釋同證明。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有醫生證明同缺席通知。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will medical certificate and absence note be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you access those records right now?
Model: 你而家開唔開到嗰啲紀錄？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，主要紀錄有齊，仲有一份文件要下載返。
Model: Okay, I have the main records. One document still needs to be downloaded.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the school parent portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經學校家長網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我正式提交之後，點知系統已經有紀錄，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。
Model: Once I lodge it, how will I know it’s on their system, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Note the lodgement reference. Allow about two weeks; during that time, keep the school informed if the absence continues.
Model: 留返電子確認紀錄。預大約兩星期；期間，如果繼續缺課，要通知學校。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果到時都仲未有消息，我係咪應該問返而家嗰份申請，定係重新開始？
Model: If I still haven’t heard anything by then, do I call about the current application, or start again?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask the school to check or correct the attendance record.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，要求學校核對或者更正出席紀錄。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住出席紀錄。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember attendance record. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: All right, I know what to do now. I’ll keep a copy of whatever I send.
Model: 好，而家我知點做喇。我交出去嘅資料都會留返副本。

## D064 — Childcare enrolment
Topic: Education

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain a childcare place. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋托兒學位。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到九月七日起每星期需要四日托兒，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that care is needed from 7 September, four days a week, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Great, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理。否則，我擔心可能錯過註冊，或者要承擔本來可以避免嘅費用。
Model: I need to deal with it before the next scheduled appointment or payment. Otherwise, I’m anxious that I may miss enrolment or become liable for an avoidable fee.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I just want to check that we’re working from the latest record.
Model: 我想確保我哋而家睇緊嘅係最新紀錄。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係呀，我手上最新就係呢份。
Model: Yes, that’s the latest one I have.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Exactly. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 係。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有入學表同家庭資料，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have enrolment form and family details, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the childcare provider’s enrolment portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經托兒服務機構嘅註冊網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 等佢哋作決定之前，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？
Model: Until they make a decision, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, keep the provider updated about the child’s start date and attendance pattern.
Model: 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約三至四星期。期間，如開始日期或者出席安排有變，要通知托兒機構。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果啲理由同實際發生嘅事對唔上，正確下一步係咩？我唔想不停打去唔同地方。
Model: If the reasons don’t match what actually happened, what’s the proper next step? I don’t want to keep ringing different people.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask the provider about enrolment and Services Australia about subsidy-specific questions.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，入學問題向托兒機構查詢，津貼個別問題向 Services Australia 查詢。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將托兒津貼，即係 CCS同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of Child Care Subsidy, or CCS with the reference number for future contact.

## D065 — TAFE course enrolment
Topic: Education

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of a TAFE course enrolment. I’ve found the enquiry. Before we get into the details, tell me what you need resolved.
Model: 我哋睇下TAFE 課程註冊嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我搵到你個查詢喇。講細節之前，你最想解決嘅係咩呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話課堂喺十月六日開始。我仍然擔心可能錯過註冊，或者要承擔本來可以避免嘅費用。
Model: Earlier I was told classes begin on 6 October. I’m still concerned that I may miss enrolment or become liable for an avoidable fee.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I can see that note. The key term on this file is Vocational Education and Training, or VET. Remember that enrolment, fees and study obligations depend on the course and cut-off dates.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係職業教育及培訓，即係 VET。要留意註冊、學費同學習責任會視乎課程同截止日期。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 下星期五之前可唔可以再幫我對一次份紀錄，睇吓有冇漏？
Model: Could you check the file once more before next Friday, just in case I’ve left something out?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to ask student services to check the enrolment, fee or course record. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係要求學生服務部核對註冊、學費或者課程紀錄。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗身份證、學習紀錄同取錄通知。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have ID, study records and enrolment offer. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the student online portal, then note the lodgement reference. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用學生網上平台提交，之後保存確認編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我交咗之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？
Model: After I submit it, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks. I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?
Model: 唔該。我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 唔該。先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。
Model: Thanks. Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the education provider’s formal review or complaint process.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，使用院校嘅正式覆核或者投訴程序。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低職業教育及培訓，即係 VET，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down Vocational Education and Training, or VET so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D066 — CSP and HECS-HELP
Topic: Education

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a student services officer. I’m calling about a university fee arrangement. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.
Model: 早晨。呢度係學生服務職員。我聯絡你係關於大學學費安排。多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我就係怕可能錯過註冊，或者要承擔本來可以避免嘅費用。我想喺今個月底之前處理好。
Model: I’m concerned I might miss enrolment or become liable for an avoidable fee. I’d like this resolved before the end of this month.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: For this matter, we need to be clear about Commonwealth Supported Place, or CSP. enrolment, fees and study obligations depend on the course and cut-off dates. Let me check how that applies to you.
Model: 呢件事最緊要先搞清楚聯邦資助學額，即係 CSP。註冊、學費同學習責任會視乎課程同截止日期。我幫你睇吓你個情況實際點樣套用。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住今個學期學生供款係四千三百澳元。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the semester student contribution is $4,300. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, ask student services to check the enrolment, fee or course record. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係要求學生服務部核對註冊、學費或者課程紀錄。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗取錄信同學費結單。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have offer letter and fee statement. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the student online portal. Upload offer letter and fee statement, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用學生網上平台。上載取錄信同學費結單，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 唔該。如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？
Model: Thanks. If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks. Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.
Model: 唔該。重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如我覺得個決定有問題，我應該搵邊個再睇一次個決定？
Model: Suppose the decision doesn’t seem right, who do I ask to look at the decision again?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, use the education provider’s formal review or complaint process.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，使用院校嘅正式覆核或者投訴程序。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，我會跟返呢個程序，唔會另外再開一個申請。
Model: All right. I’ll follow that process rather than starting another request.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That helps a lot. I’ll get the paperwork organised this afternoon.
Model: 幫到好多呀。我今日下晝會整理好啲文件。

## D067 — Census date withdrawal
Topic: Education

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a student services officer. I can see an enquiry about a course withdrawal. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟學生服務職員。我見到有一個關於退科問題嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到學籍及費用責任截止日，即係 census date。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions census date, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because enrolment, fees and study obligations depend on the course and cut-off dates. Your current record also says the census date is 31 August.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為註冊、學費同學習責任會視乎課程同截止日期。你目前紀錄亦顯示census date 係八月三十一日。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺通知所列日期之前知道，因為可能錯過註冊，或者要承擔本來可以避免嘅費用。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the date shown on the notice, because I may miss enrolment or become liable for an avoidable fee.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, ask student services to check the enrolment, fee or course record.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要要求學生服務部核對註冊、學費或者課程紀錄。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗註冊紀錄同退科申請。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered enrolment record and withdrawal request. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?
Model: 好，相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 你頭先講嗰啲差唔多有齊，淨係仲有一份喺屋企。
Model: I have almost everything you mentioned; one record is still at home.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the student online portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用學生網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我今日搞掂晒啲文件，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
Model: If I get the paperwork in today, what happens while the matter is being checked?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about two weeks. While you wait, continue attending classes unless the provider tells you otherwise, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約兩星期。等候期間，除非院校另有通知，否則繼續上堂，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果個申請畀人拒絕，我可唔可以睇咗理由先，再決定使唔使申請覆核？
Model: If the application is refused, can I see the reasons before deciding whether to seek a review?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, use the education provider’s formal review or complaint process.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，使用院校嘅正式覆核或者投訴程序。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返學籍及費用責任截止日，即係 census date呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use census date when I contact the service again.

## D068 — USI for training
Topic: Education

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a training enrolment. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進培訓註冊。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係迎新安排喺九月十一日。我擔心可能錯過註冊，或者要承擔本來可以避免嘅費用。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that orientation is on 11 September. My main worry is that I could miss enrolment or become liable for an avoidable fee.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Alright, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理唔到，實際會有咩後果？
Model: What are the consequences if I can’t resolve this by the next scheduled appointment or payment?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, ask student services to check the enrolment, fee or course record. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，要求學生服務部核對註冊、學費或者課程紀錄。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有有相身份證同現有 USI 資料。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will photo ID and existing USI details be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you show me the supporting records today?
Model: 你今日可唔可以畀我睇吓啲證明資料？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分都喺度，最後嗰份我今晚可以補交。
Model: Most are here; I can send the last one tonight.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the student online portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經學生網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我上載完會唔會有嘢證明成功咗，我想有個編號，之後跟進都有嘢可以講。
Model: Will I get anything showing the upload went through, I want something I can refer to if I need to follow it up.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Save the online confirmation. Allow about three to four weeks; during that time, continue attending classes unless the provider tells you otherwise.
Model: 保存網上確認資料。預大約三至四星期；期間，除非院校另有通知，否則繼續上堂。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果應該有回覆嗰陣都冇聲氣，點樣跟進先唔會變咗重複申請？
Model: If the due time passes without a response, what’s the right way to chase it up without duplicating the request?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, use the education provider’s formal review or complaint process.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，使用院校嘅正式覆核或者投訴程序。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住唯一學生識別號碼，即係 USI。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Unique Student Identifier, or USI. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Perfect. I’ll sort it out and keep the confirmation for later.
Model: 好極喇。我會處理好，亦會留返確認紀錄。

## D069 — Unauthorised card transaction
Topic: Financial

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain a disputed card transaction. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋有爭議嘅信用卡交易。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到琴晚有一筆二百八十六個四毫澳元收費，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that the charge is $286.40 from last night, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I see. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺下星期五之前處理。否則，我擔心可能有金錢損失，或者要畀額外費用同利息。
Model: I need to deal with it before next Friday. Otherwise, I’m worried that money may be lost or extra fees or interest may apply.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you confirm there hasn’t been another notice since then?
Model: 可唔可以確認之後冇再收到另一封通知？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係呀，我嚟之前都有再確認。
Model: Yes. I checked before I came in.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That helps. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 咁就清楚喇。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有月結單同交易截圖，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have statement and transaction screenshot, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Are the supporting documents handy at the moment?
Model: 啲證明文件而家喺唔喺手邊？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分證明都帶咗，仲有一樣喺第二度。
Model: I’ve got most of the evidence with me; one item is elsewhere.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through secure online banking and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經安全網上銀行上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 佢哋未核對完份紀錄之前，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？
Model: Before they finish reviewing the record, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Great, I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?
Model: 好，我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。
Model: Great, Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then use the bank’s internal dispute process, then AFCA if the matter is eligible.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，先用銀行內部爭議程序，如個案合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將未經授權交易同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of unauthorised transaction with the reference number for future contact.

## D070 — Direct debit cancellation
Topic: Financial

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of a direct debit cancellation. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
Model: 我哋睇下取消自動扣賬嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話下一筆七十四澳元扣款星期五到期。我仍然擔心可能有金錢損失，或者要畀額外費用同利息。
Model: Earlier I was told the next $74 debit is due on Friday. I’m still concerned that money may be lost or extra fees or interest may apply.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: For this matter, we need to be clear about direct debit. Remember that transactions and account features must be checked against the specific product terms. Let me check how that applies to you.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係自動扣賬。要留意交易同戶口功能要按實際產品條款核對。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 去到今個月底之前，你可唔可以幫我睇吓仲欠唔欠資料？
Model: Before the end of this month, can you check whether you still need anything from me?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to ask the bank to investigate the transaction or account setting. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係要求銀行調查交易或者戶口設定。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗戶口結單同商戶通知。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have account statement and merchant notice. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use secure online banking, then keep the electronic acknowledgement. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用安全網上銀行提交，之後記低提交參考編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 佢哋收齊資料之後，我應該等幾耐先再打電話？
Model: Once they have everything, how long should I leave it before I call?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about seven to ten business days. During that period, monitor the account and keep all transaction records, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約七至十個工作日。期間，留意戶口情況並保留所有交易紀錄，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如評估期間我有一項資料改咗，我係咪應該即刻通知佢哋？
Model: Suppose one of my details changes during the assessment, should I tell them straight away?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the bank’s internal dispute process, then AFCA if the matter is eligible.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，先用銀行內部爭議程序，如個案合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低自動扣賬，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down direct debit so I can refer to the matter accurately.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: One more thing: should I keep the confirmation number with the original documents?
Model: 仲有一樣：個確認編號係咪應該同原本文件一齊留低？

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係，一齊保存。之後如果要跟進，就可以證明你交過咩同埋幾時交。
Model: Yes. Keep them together so you can show what was submitted and when, if you need to follow it up later.

## D071 — BPAY payment reference
Topic: Financial

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a bank customer service officer. I’m calling about a BPAY payment problem. All right, I’ve got the matter on screen. What do you need help with first?
Model: 早晨。呢度係銀行客戶服務職員。我聯絡你係關於BPAY 付款問題。好，我而家睇到件事嘅資料。你想我先幫你處理邊一部分？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有啲驚會有金錢損失，或者要畀額外費用同利息。我想喺通知所列日期之前處理好。
Model: My concern is that money may be lost or extra fees or interest may apply. I’d like this resolved before the date shown on the notice.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The key term is BPAY customer reference number. Generally, transactions and account features must be checked against the specific product terms. We must check your exact facts.
Model: 關鍵用語係BPAY 客戶參考號碼。一般嚟講，交易同戶口功能要按實際產品條款核對。我哋要核實你嘅實際資料。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住琴日下午付款五百一十澳元。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says $510 was paid yesterday afternoon. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, ask the bank to investigate the transaction or account setting. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係要求銀行調查交易或者戶口設定。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗付款收據同賬單。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have payment receipt and bill. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use secure online banking. Upload payment receipt and bill, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用安全網上銀行。上載付款收據同賬單，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？
Model: Exactly. I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Exactly. Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.
Model: 係。先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 嗰筆卡交易仲顯示 pending。我應該而家提出爭議，定係等佢正式過數先？
Model: The card transaction is still showing as pending. Should I lodge a dispute now or wait until it is completed?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Report it to the bank now if you do not recognise it. The bank can explain whether it can investigate while the transaction is pending.
Model: 如果你唔認得嗰筆交易，而家就通知銀行。銀行會解釋交易仲 pending 時可唔可以開始調查。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好極喇。我會處理好，亦會留返確認紀錄。
Model: Perfect. I’ll sort it out and keep the confirmation for later.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good, I’ll do that. If anything changes, I’ll let the service know straight away.
Model: 好呀，我會照做。如果有任何改動，我會即刻通知返個機構。

## D072 — Credit-card interest
Topic: Financial

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a bank customer service officer. I can see an enquiry about a credit-card interest charge. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟銀行客戶服務職員。我見到有一個關於信用卡利息收費嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到免息期。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions interest-free period, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because transactions and account features must be checked against the specific product terms. Your current record also says the statement shows $48.70 interest.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為交易同戶口功能要按實際產品條款核對。你目前紀錄亦顯示月結單顯示四十八個七毫澳元利息。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前知道，因為可能有金錢損失，或者要畀額外費用同利息。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the next scheduled appointment or payment, because money may be lost or extra fees or interest may apply.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, ask the bank to investigate the transaction or account setting.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要要求銀行調查交易或者戶口設定。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗兩期月結單同付款收據。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered two statements and payment receipt. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Are those supporting papers with you now?
Model: 啲證明文件而家有冇喺身？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分帶咗，不過有一頁留咗喺屋企。
Model: I brought most of it, but one page is at home.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use secure online banking unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用安全網上銀行。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我正式交咗資料之後，件事核對緊嗰段時間會點㗎？
Model: After I lodge the information, what happens while the matter is being checked?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about three to four weeks. While you wait, monitor the account and keep all transaction records, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約三至四星期。等候期間，留意戶口情況並保留所有交易紀錄，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如個機構最後唔批，我可唔可以睇咗理由先，再決定使唔使申請覆核？
Model: Suppose the service doesn’t approve it, can I see the reasons before deciding whether to seek a review?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, use the bank’s internal dispute process, then AFCA if the matter is eligible.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，先用銀行內部爭議程序，如個案合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返免息期呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use interest-free period when I contact the service again.

## D073 — Mortgage offset account
Topic: Financial

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a home-loan offset account. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進按揭對沖戶口。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係對沖戶口大約有三萬五千澳元。我擔心可能有金錢損失，或者要畀額外費用同利息。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that the offset balance is about $35,000. My concern is that money may be lost or extra fees or interest may apply.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Got it. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我可能趕唔切下星期五之前搞掂，咁會唔會有問題？
Model: I may not get this done by next Friday. Will that cause a problem?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, ask the bank to investigate the transaction or account setting. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，要求銀行調查交易或者戶口設定。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有按揭結單同產品資料。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will loan statement and product details be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Have you brought the records we just discussed?
Model: 頭先講嗰啲紀錄你有冇帶嚟？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分都有副本，仲欠一份資料。
Model: I have copies of most of them; one is still outstanding.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through secure online banking. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經安全網上銀行提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我上載完會唔會有嘢證明成功咗，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。
Model: Will I get anything showing the upload went through, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Save the confirmation number. Allow about five business days; during that time, monitor the account and keep all transaction records.
Model: 保存網上確認資料。預大約五個工作日；期間，留意戶口情況並保留所有交易紀錄。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果應該有回覆嗰陣都冇聲氣，點樣跟進先唔會變咗重複申請？
Model: If the due time passes without a response, what’s the right way to chase it up without duplicating the request?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
