Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, use the bank’s internal dispute process, then AFCA if the matter is eligible.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，先用銀行內部爭議程序，如個案合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住對沖戶口。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember offset account. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Right, I’ll keep an eye on the messages and save any new notice I receive.
Model: 明白，我會留意訊息，收到任何新通知都會保存。

## D074 — Home-loan redraw
Topic: Financial

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain a home-loan redraw request. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋按揭提取多還款項要求。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到想提取嘅金額係六千澳元，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that the requested redraw amount is $6,000, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Sounds good. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺今個月底之前處理。否則，我擔心可能有金錢損失，或者要畀額外費用同利息。
Model: I need to deal with it before the end of this month. Otherwise, I’m worried that money may be lost or extra fees or interest may apply.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can I treat what you just told me as the current information?
Model: 咁我可以按你頭先講嗰份資料去處理？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 啱，呢個資料而家仲有效。
Model: Yes. The information is still current.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: All right. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有按揭戶口同提取條件，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have loan account and redraw conditions, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you access the evidence we just mentioned?
Model: 頭先提過嗰啲證明你而家開唔開到？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 有，淨係仲有一份文件要遲啲先拎到。
Model: Yes. There’s just one document I need to bring later.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through secure online banking and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經安全網上銀行上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 等佢哋作決定之前，有冇啲費用或者實際影響我應該知道？
Model: Until they make a decision, is there a cost or practical consequence I should know about?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Great, If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?
Model: 好，如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。
Model: Great, Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then use the bank’s internal dispute process, then AFCA if the matter is eligible.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，先用銀行內部爭議程序，如個案合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將提取多還款項功能同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of redraw facility with the reference number for future contact.

## D075 — Financial hardship
Topic: Financial

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of loan repayment difficulty. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.
Model: 我哋睇下貸款還款困難嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話困難可能維持大約三個月。我仍然擔心可能有金錢損失，或者要畀額外費用同利息。
Model: Earlier I was told the difficulty may last about three months. I’m still concerned that money may be lost or extra fees or interest may apply.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is financial hardship assistance. Remember that transactions and account features must be checked against the specific product terms. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係財務困難援助。要留意交易同戶口功能要按實際產品條款核對。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 通知所列日期之前可唔可以再幫我對一次份紀錄，睇吓有冇漏？
Model: Could you check the file once more before the date shown on the notice, just in case I’ve left something out?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to ask the bank to investigate the transaction or account setting. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係要求銀行調查交易或者戶口設定。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗收入資料同現時開支。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have income details and current expenses. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use secure online banking, then keep the electronic acknowledgement. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用安全網上銀行提交，之後保存網上確認資料。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 啲文件交齊之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？
Model: Once the documents are in, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about two weeks. During that period, monitor the account and keep all transaction records, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約兩星期。期間，留意戶口情況並保留所有交易紀錄，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果件事完成之前有重要變更，點樣更新會最穩陣？
Model: If there’s a material change before the matter is finalised, what’s the safest way to update it?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the bank’s internal dispute process, then AFCA if the matter is eligible.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，先用銀行內部爭議程序，如個案合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低財務困難援助，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down financial hardship assistance so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D076 — Tax return refund
Topic: Financial

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with an ATO service officer. I’m calling about a delayed tax refund. I’ve found the enquiry. Before we get into the details, tell me what you need resolved.
Model: 早晨。呢度係澳洲稅務局服務職員。我聯絡你係關於延遲退稅。我搵到你個查詢喇。講細節之前，你最想解決嘅係咩呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我最擔心就係可能有金錢損失，或者要畀額外費用同利息。我想喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理好。
Model: My concern is that money may be lost or extra fees or interest may apply. I’d like this resolved before the next scheduled appointment or payment.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is income tax assessment. tax-return and refund details should be checked against the ATO assessment and nominated payment information. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 呢件事最緊要先搞清楚入息稅評稅通知。報稅同退稅資料應按 ATO 評稅同已登記付款資料核對。我幫你睇吓你個情況實際點樣套用。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住預計退稅係一千三百四十澳元。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the expected refund is $1,340. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, check the notice of assessment and nominated bank details in the ATO-linked account. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係喺已連結 ATO 嘅帳戶核對評稅通知同登記銀行資料。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗評稅通知同銀行資料。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have notice of assessment and bank details. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use your myGov-linked ATO online service. Upload notice of assessment and bank details, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用你經 myGov 連結嘅 ATO 網上服務。上載評稅通知同銀行資料，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 通常要等幾耐？我擔心可能有金錢損失，或者要畀額外費用同利息。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
Model: How long should I wait? I’m worried that money may be lost or extra fees or interest may apply. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, keep the assessment notice and monitor official ATO messages. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
Model: 預大約三至四星期。期間，保留評稅通知並留意 ATO 正式訊息。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我睇完都覺得個決定唔啱，係咪有正式嘅覆核程序可以跟？
Model: If I still think the decision is wrong, is there a formal review process I can use?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask the ATO to explain the payment status or available review pathway.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，要求 ATO 解釋付款狀況或者可用覆核途徑。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，我會咁樣做，亦會留返提交日期嘅證明。
Model: Fine, I’ll do it that way and keep proof of the date I sent it.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Fine, I’ll do it that way and keep proof of the date I sent it.
Model: 好，我會咁樣做，亦會留返提交日期嘅證明。

## D077 — Rental application
Topic: Housing

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a property management officer. I can see an enquiry about a rental application. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟物業管理職員。我見到有一個關於租屋申請嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到租屋申請。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions rental application, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. Your current record also says the weekly rent is $590 for a twelve-month lease.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。你目前紀錄亦顯示週租五百九十澳元，租期十二個月。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下星期五之前知道，因為可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before next Friday, because I may pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗工作證明同租屋推薦資料。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered employment evidence and rental references. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Right, Can you pull up those documents on your phone?
Model: 明白，你可唔可以而家用電話開返啲文件？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 有，大部分都喺度，仲要攞多一份文件。
Model: Yes, most of it. I still need to collect one document.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the tenancy online portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用租務網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假設我今日下晝上載晒，評估期間仲有冇啲咩要特別留意？
Model: Say I upload all of this this afternoon, is there anything else I need to watch while it’s under assessment?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about five business days. While you wait, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約五個工作日。等候期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如個機構最後唔批，佢哋會唔會講明係申請邊一部分出問題？
Model: Suppose the service doesn’t approve it, will they tell me what part of the application caused the problem?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返租屋申請呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use rental application when I contact the service again.

## D078 — Bond and condition report
Topic: Housing

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on move-in tenancy paperwork. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進入住租務文件。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係按金二千三百六十澳元，九月一日入住。我擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that the bond is $2,360 and move-in is 1 September. What worries me is that I may pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: For this matter, we need to be clear about condition report. In this type of matter, tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. Let me check how that applies to you.
Model: 決定下一步之前，要記住物業狀況報告呢個詞。喺呢類事情，租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我可能趕唔切今個月底之前搞掂，咁會唔會有問題？
Model: I may not get this done by the end of this month. Will that cause a problem?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有按金收據、租約同物業狀況報告。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will bond receipt, lease and condition report be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Are you able to show me those records today?
Model: 你今日方唔方便畀我睇嗰啲紀錄？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 有呀，不過今朝有一樣點都搵唔到。
Model: Yes, except for one item I couldn’t find this morning.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the tenancy online portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經租務網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我正式提交之後，點知系統已經有紀錄，如果之後有爭議，嗰個日期可能好重要㗎。
Model: Once I lodge it, how will I know it’s on their system, That date could matter if there’s a dispute later.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Exactly. If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?
Model: 係。如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。
Model: Exactly. Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住物業狀況報告。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember condition report. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, All right, I know what to do now. I’ll keep a copy of whatever I send.
Model: 好，好，而家我知點做喇。我交出去嘅資料都會留返副本。

## D079 — Urgent repairs
Topic: Housing

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain an urgent repair request. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋緊急維修要求。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到由琴晚開始一直冇熱水，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that there has been no hot water since last night, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That’s clear. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺通知所列日期之前處理。否則，我擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。
Model: I need to deal with it before the date shown on the notice. Otherwise, My worry is that I might pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Just to confirm, that’s the latest information you have?
Model: 我想確認一下，你手上嗰份係咪最新資料？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係呀，之後一直冇改過。
Model: That’s right; nothing has changed since then.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: No problem. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 冇問題。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有相片同書面維修要求，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have photos and written repair request, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Have you brought the records we just discussed?
Model: 頭先講嗰啲紀錄你有冇帶嚟？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 有，大部分都喺度，仲要攞多一份文件。
Model: Yes, most of it. I still need to collect one document.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the tenancy online portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經租務網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 等佢哋作決定之前，仲有冇其他情況我應該早啲有準備？
Model: Until they make a decision, could anything else happen that I need to prepare for?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records.
Model: 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約兩星期。期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果封決定信我睇完都覺得有錯，我下一步應該搵邊個，先唔使又打同一輪電話？
Model: If I read the decision and still think it’s wrong, who should I contact next instead of making the same phone call again?

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將緊急維修同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of urgent repair with the reference number for future contact.

## D080 — Rent increase notice
Topic: Housing

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of a rent increase notice. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
Model: 我哋睇下加租通知嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話十月二十日起週租可能由五百四十加到六百一十澳元。我仍然擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。
Model: Earlier I was told rent may rise from $540 to $610 a week on 20 October. I’m still concerned that I may pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is rent increase notice. Remember that tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係加租通知。要留意租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前可唔可以再幫我對一次份紀錄，睇吓有冇漏？
Model: Could you check the file once more before the next scheduled appointment or payment, just in case I’ve left something out?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗租約同加租通知。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have tenancy agreement and increase notice. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the tenancy online portal, then note the lodgement reference. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用租務網上平台提交，之後保存確認編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我交咗之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？
Model: After I submit it, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about three to four weeks. During that period, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約三至四星期。期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果件事完成之前有重要變更，點樣更新會最穩陣？
Model: If there’s a material change before the matter is finalised, what’s the safest way to update it?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低加租通知，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down rent increase notice so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D081 — Ending a tenancy
Topic: Housing

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a property management officer. I’m calling about a notice to end a tenancy. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
Model: 早晨。呢度係物業管理職員。我聯絡你係關於終止租約通知。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 而家我最憂心係可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。我想喺下星期五之前處理好。
Model: I’m concerned I might pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline. I’d like this resolved before next Friday.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The part that matters here is termination notice. tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
Model: 我哋而家要睇終止租約通知。租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。我即刻幫你對一對件紀錄。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住預計搬走日期係九月三十日。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the proposed move-out date is 30 September. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗租約同準備發出嘅書面通知。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have lease and proposed written notice. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the tenancy online portal. Upload lease and proposed written notice, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用租務網上平台。上載租約同準備發出嘅書面通知，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？
Model: Alright, I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Alright, Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.
Model: 好，先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果個結果我點睇都覺得唔對路，我應該搵邊個再睇一次個決定？
Model: If the result still looks wrong to me, who do I ask to look at the decision again?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白。我會留返確認紀錄，有需要先再跟進。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the confirmation and follow up only if I need to.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, Good, I’ll do that. If anything changes, I’ll let the service know straight away.
Model: 好，好呀，我會照做。如果有任何改動，我會即刻通知返個機構。

## D082 — Breaking a fixed-term lease
Topic: Housing

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a property management officer. I can see an enquiry about leaving a fixed-term lease early. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟物業管理職員。我見到有一個關於提早離開定期租約嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到提前終止租約。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions break lease, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. Your current record also says four months remain on the fixed term.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。你目前紀錄亦顯示定期租約仲有四個月。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺今個月底之前知道，因為可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the end of this month, because I may pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗租約同預計搬走日期。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered lease and proposed move-out date. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Did you bring the paperwork we talked about?
Model: 頭先講嗰啲文件你有冇帶埋嚟？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 有呀，不過仲有一份紀錄我要再搵吓。
Model: Yes, apart from one record I still need to find.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the tenancy online portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用租務網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假設我今日下晝上載晒，等緊期間有冇啲嘢一定要做或者唔好做呀？
Model: Say I upload all of this this afternoon, is there anything I should or shouldn’t do while I’m waiting?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about seven to ten business days. While you wait, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約七至十個工作日。等候期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我收到一個負面決定，我可唔可以叫佢哋講清楚點解，再決定係咪覆核？
Model: If I get a negative decision, can I ask them to explain exactly why before I challenge it?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返提前終止租約呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use break lease when I contact the service again.

## D083 — Bond refund dispute
Topic: Housing

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a bond refund dispute. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進按金退款爭議。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係建議清潔扣款係四百二十澳元。我擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that the proposed cleaning deduction is $420. I’m nervous that I might pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: For this matter, we need to be clear about bond claim. In this type of matter, tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. Let me check how that applies to you.
Model: 決定下一步之前，要記住按金申索呢個詞。喺呢類事情，租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果通知所列日期之前都搞唔掂，問題會有幾大呀？
Model: How serious is it if I’m not able to fix this before the date shown on the notice?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有狀況報告、相片同清潔收據。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will condition reports, photos and cleaning receipt be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Are the supporting documents handy at the moment?
Model: 啲證明文件而家喺唔喺手邊？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分都有副本，仲欠一份資料。
Model: I have copies of most of them; one is still outstanding.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the tenancy online portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經租務網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我正式提交之後，點知系統已經有紀錄，我想留返日期證明，費事之後為限期拗數。
Model: Once I lodge it, how will I know it’s on their system, I’d like to keep proof of the date in case the deadline becomes an issue.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the submission receipt. Allow about two weeks; during that time, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records.
Model: 保存網上確認資料。預大約兩星期；期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果過咗嗰段時間都冇變化，我應該跟返原本個申請，定係再交一份？
Model: What if that timeframe passes and nothing changes, should I follow up the existing request or submit another one?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住按金申索。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember bond claim. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

## D084 — Routine inspection
Topic: Housing

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain a routine rental inspection. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋例行租屋檢查。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到檢查安排喺星期二朝早十點，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that the inspection is booked for Tuesday at 10 am, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Fair enough. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理。否則，我擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。
Model: I need to deal with it before the next scheduled appointment or payment. Otherwise, I’m a bit worried I’ll pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can I treat what you just told me as the current information?
Model: 咁我可以按你頭先講嗰份資料去處理？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 呢個而家仲係最新資料。
Model: That’s still the current information.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 唔該，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有檢查通知同工作更表，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have inspection notice and work roster, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Did you bring the paperwork we talked about?
Model: 頭先講嗰啲文件你有冇帶埋嚟？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要嗰幾份有，一個檔案仲喺屋企部電腦。
Model: I have the main ones. One file is on my home computer.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the tenancy online portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經租務網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 件事仲處理緊嗰陣，會唔會有額外收費或者其他後果㗎？
Model: While this is still being sorted out, could I be charged anything extra or face another consequence?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about three to four weeks. Meanwhile, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records.
Model: 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約三至四星期。期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果啲理由同實際發生嘅事對唔上，我要點樣跟正式程序提出覆核？
Model: If the reasons don’t match what actually happened, how do I challenge it through the right process?

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將入屋通知同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of entry notice with the reference number for future contact.

## D085 — Shared utility bill
Topic: Housing

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of a shared electricity charge. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
Model: 我哋睇下分攤電費嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話要求分攤二百三十澳元。我仍然擔心可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。
Model: Earlier I was told the requested contribution is $230. I’m still concerned that I may pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The part that matters here is utility apportionment. Remember that tenancy procedures depend on the state or territory and the agreement. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係公用事業費用分攤。要留意租務程序會視乎所在州或領地同租約。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 下星期五之前可唔可以再幫我對一次份紀錄，睇吓有冇漏？
Model: Could you check the file once more before next Friday, just in case I’ve left something out?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to write to the agent or rental provider and keep the tenancy evidence. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係書面聯絡地產代理或者出租方，並保留租務證據。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗賬單副本同租約。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have bill copy and tenancy agreement. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the tenancy online portal, then note the lodgement reference. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用租務網上平台提交，之後留返提交收據。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我交咗之後，我應該等幾耐先再打電話？
Model: After I submit it, how long should I leave it before I call?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about five business days. During that period, keep paying undisputed rent and keep written tenancy records, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約五個工作日。期間，繼續交冇爭議嘅租金，並保留書面租務紀錄，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我等緊期間有重要資料改咗，我係咪應該即刻通知佢哋？
Model: What if something important changes while I’m waiting, should I tell them straight away?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the relevant state tenancy service or tribunal process if needed.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，有需要時使用所在州嘅租務服務或者審裁程序。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低公用事業費用分攤，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down utility apportionment so I can refer to the matter accurately.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: One more thing: should I keep the confirmation number with the original documents?
Model: 仲有一樣：個確認編號係咪應該同原本文件一齊留低？

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係，一齊保存。之後如果要跟進，就可以證明你交過咩同埋幾時交。
Model: Yes. Keep them together so you can show what was submitted and when, if you need to follow it up later.

## D086 — NSW tenancy tribunal
Topic: Housing

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a New South Wales tenancy information officer. I’m calling about a New South Wales tenancy dispute. Please confirm your full name and reference number before we continue.
Model: 早晨。呢度係新南威爾士州租務資訊職員。我聯絡你係關於新州租務爭議。繼續之前，請確認你嘅全名同參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我就係怕可能要多交租，或者錯過重要租務限期。我想喺今個月底之前處理好。
Model: My main worry is that I could pay extra rent or miss an important tenancy deadline. I’d like this resolved before the end of this month.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The key term is NSW Civil and Administrative Tribunal, or NCAT. Generally, the appropriate NSW tenancy dispute pathway depends on the issue, evidence and procedural requirements. We must check your exact facts.
Model: 關鍵用語係新州民事及行政審裁處，即係 NCAT。一般嚟講，合適嘅新州租務爭議途徑會視乎問題、證據同程序要求。我哋要核實你嘅實際資料。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住第一次維修要求係六星期前發出。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the first repair request was sent six weeks ago. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, organise the tenancy evidence and check the appropriate NSW dispute pathway. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係整理租務證據並核實合適嘅新州爭議處理途徑。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗租約、維修要求同相片。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have lease, repair requests and photos. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the relevant NSW tenancy or tribunal online service. Upload lease, repair requests and photos, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用相關新州租務或者審裁網上服務。上載租約、維修要求同相片，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？
Model: Alright, If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Alright, Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.
Model: 好，重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如我覺得個決定有問題，我可唔可以先要求覆核，再決定使唔使投訴呀？
Model: Suppose the decision doesn’t seem right, can I ask for a review before making a complaint?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask the relevant tenancy service about the next step, including NCAT where appropriate.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，向相關租務服務查詢下一步，包括適用時嘅 NCAT 程序。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 可以，好，我今日就處理，啲文件亦會放埋一齊。
Model: Sure, Okay, I’ll deal with that today and keep the paperwork together.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for explaining it. I’ll wait for the update and keep the records together.
Model: 唔該你解釋。我會等更新，同時將啲紀錄整理好。

## D087 — Motor claim excess
Topic: Insurance

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling an insurance claims officer. I can see an enquiry about a motor-insurance claim. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟保險索償主任。我見到有一個關於汽車保險索償嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到保單自付額。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions policy excess, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because cover depends on the policy wording, limits, exclusions and any applicable excess. Your current record also says the quoted excess is $850.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為保障視乎保單條款、上限、不保事項同適用自付額。你目前紀錄亦顯示所列自付額係八百五十澳元。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺通知所列日期之前知道，因為索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the date shown on the notice, because the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, provide the available evidence and ask the insurer to review the claim.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要提供現有證據，並要求保險公司覆核索償。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗保單資料表同索償通知。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered policy schedule and claim notice. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Right, Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?
Model: 明白，相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 有呀，不過今朝有一樣點都搵唔到。
Model: Yes, except for one item I couldn’t find this morning.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the insurer claims portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用保險公司索償網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我交咗啲文件之後，我係咪淨係等就得，定係仲有啲嘢要繼續做？
Model: Once I’ve submitted the documents, do I just wait, or is there something I need to keep doing?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about two weeks. While you wait, take reasonable steps to prevent further loss and keep evidence, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約兩星期。等候期間，採取合理措施防止損失擴大，並保留證據，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果佢哋最後話唔得，佢哋會唔會講明係申請邊一部分出問題？
Model: If they eventually say no, will they tell me what part of the application caused the problem?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, use internal dispute resolution, then AFCA if the complaint is eligible.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，先用內部爭議處理程序，如投訴合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返保單自付額呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use policy excess when I contact the service again.

## D088 — Storm damage at home
Topic: Insurance

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a home-insurance claim after a storm. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進暴風雨後家居保險索償。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係暴風雨喺星期六晚發生。我擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that the storm occurred on Saturday night. My concern is that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is make-safe work. In this type of matter, cover depends on the policy wording, limits, exclusions and any applicable excess. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 決定下一步之前，要記住防止損失擴大嘅緊急工程呢個詞。喺呢類事情，保障視乎保單條款、上限、不保事項同適用自付額。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 去到下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期件事仲未解決，我應該預咩情況？
Model: If the next scheduled appointment or payment arrives and this is still unresolved, what should I expect?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, provide the available evidence and ask the insurer to review the claim. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，提供現有證據，並要求保險公司覆核索償。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有相片、維修報價同索償編號。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will photos, repair quote and claim number be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you access those records right now?
Model: 你而家開唔開到嗰啲紀錄？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 差唔多全部有，不過仲有一樣冇帶喺身。
Model: I’ve got nearly everything, but one item isn’t with me.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the insurer claims portal. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經保險公司索償網上平台提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我點樣先知佢哋真係收到啲文件，我唔想遲啲先發現原來啲文件根本冇收到。
Model: How can I tell they’ve actually received the documents, I don’t want to find out later that the files never arrived.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Save the online confirmation. Allow about three to four weeks; during that time, take reasonable steps to prevent further loss and keep evidence.
Model: 保存確認編號。預大約三至四星期；期間，採取合理措施防止損失擴大，並保留證據。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果應該有回覆嗰陣都冇聲氣，我係咪應該問返而家嗰份申請，定係重新開始？
Model: If the due time passes without a response, do I call about the current application, or start again?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, use internal dispute resolution, then AFCA if the complaint is eligible.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，先用內部爭議處理程序，如投訴合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住防止損失擴大嘅緊急工程。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember make-safe work. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

## D089 — Travel insurance claim
Topic: Insurance

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain a travel-insurance claim. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋旅遊保險索償。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到額外住宿費係六百八十澳元，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that extra accommodation cost $680, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺下星期五之前處理。否則，我擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。
Model: I need to deal with it before next Friday. Otherwise, I’m worried that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Just checking: that’s still the latest version you received?
Model: 確認一下，呢個係你最近收到嗰個版本？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 呢個而家仲係最新資料。
Model: That’s still the current information.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有航空公司通知、收據同行程表，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have airline notice, receipts and itinerary, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you access the evidence we just mentioned?
Model: 頭先提過嗰啲證明你而家開唔開到？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 重要嗰啲喺晒度，淨係欠一份證明。
Model: The important ones are here. I’m missing one supporting record.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the insurer claims portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經保險公司索償網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 佢哋未核對完份紀錄之前，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？
Model: Before they finish reviewing the record, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I see. I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?
Model: 明白。我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白。先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。
Model: I see. Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then use internal dispute resolution, then AFCA if the complaint is eligible.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，先用內部爭議處理程序，如投訴合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將受保事件同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of covered event with the reference number for future contact.

## D090 — Private health waiting period
Topic: Insurance

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of private-health cover before treatment. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.
Model: 我哋睇下治療前私人醫療保險保障嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話手術預約係十一月四日。我仍然擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。
Model: Earlier I was told the procedure is booked for 4 November. I’m still concerned that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is waiting period. Remember that waiting periods and benefits depend on the policy, level of cover and treatment category. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係等候期。要留意等候期同保障金額會視乎保單、保障級別同治療類別。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 今個月底之前可唔可以再幫我對一次份紀錄，睇吓有冇漏？
Model: Could you check the file once more before the end of this month, just in case I’ve left something out?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to ask the insurer to confirm in writing whether a waiting period applies to the planned treatment. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係要求保險公司書面確認計劃治療係咪適用等候期。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗保單資料同醫院報價。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have policy details and hospital quote. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use the insurer’s member portal, then save the confirmation number. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用保險公司會員網上平台提交，之後保存確認編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我交咗之後，大概幾時再問吓會比較合理？
Model: After I submit it, what’s a sensible time to ask for an update?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I see. If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?
Model: 明白。如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白。重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。
Model: I see. Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, use the insurer complaint process and ask about the appropriate external complaints body if unresolved.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，先用保險公司投訴程序，如未解決再查詢合適外部投訴機構。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低等候期，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down waiting period so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D091 — Premium renewal
Topic: Insurance

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with an insurance claims officer. I’m calling about an insurance renewal premium. All right, I’ve got the matter on screen. What do you need help with first?
Model: 早晨。呢度係保險索償主任。我聯絡你係關於保險續保保費。好，我而家睇到件事嘅資料。你想我先幫你處理邊一部分？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我最擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。我想喺通知所列日期之前處理好。
Model: My concern is that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid. I’d like this resolved before the date shown on the notice.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The part that matters here is insurance premium. cover depends on the policy wording, limits, exclusions and any applicable excess. I’ll go through your situation rather than assume it’s the same as someone else’s.
Model: 我哋而家要睇保費。保障視乎保單條款、上限、不保事項同適用自付額。我即刻幫你對一對件紀錄。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住全年保費由一千二百四十加到一千五百二十澳元。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the annual premium rose from $1,240 to $1,520. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, provide the available evidence and ask the insurer to review the claim. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係提供現有證據，並要求保險公司覆核索償。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗續保通知同保單資料表。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have renewal notice and policy schedule. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use the insurer claims portal. Upload renewal notice and policy schedule, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用保險公司索償網上平台。上載續保通知同保單資料表，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 通常要等幾耐？我擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。如果聽日都冇消息，我係咪應該再打電話？
Model: How long should I wait? I’m worried that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid. Should I call again if I hear nothing tomorrow?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, take reasonable steps to prevent further loss and keep evidence. Don’t submit the same request twice unless asked.
Model: 預大約兩星期。期間，採取合理措施防止損失擴大，並保留證據。除非有人要求，否則唔好重複提交同一要求。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果個結果我點睇都覺得唔對路，係咪有正式嘅覆核程序可以跟？
Model: If the result still looks wrong to me, is there a formal review process I can use?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, use internal dispute resolution, then AFCA if the complaint is eligible.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，先用內部爭議處理程序，如投訴合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 唔該。我提交之前會再對一次啲資料。
Model: Thank you. I’ll check the details once more before I submit anything.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That makes sense. I’ll sort out the missing item and keep the reference number.
Model: 咁就明喇。我會補返欠嗰份資料，再留低個參考編號。

## D092 — Contents theft claim
Topic: Insurance

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling an insurance claims officer. I can see an enquiry about a contents-theft claim. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟保險索償主任。我見到有一個關於家居財物盜竊索償嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到物品擁有證明。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions proof of ownership, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because cover depends on the policy wording, limits, exclusions and any applicable excess. Your current record also says the estimated loss is about $3,700.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為保障視乎保單條款、上限、不保事項同適用自付額。你目前紀錄亦顯示估計損失大約三千七百澳元。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前知道，因為索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before the next scheduled appointment or payment, because the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, provide the available evidence and ask the insurer to review the claim.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要提供現有證據，並要求保險公司覆核索償。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗警方事故編號、相片同銀行紀錄。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered police event number, photos and bank records. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, Can you show me the supporting records today?
Model: 好，你今日可唔可以畀我睇吓啲證明資料？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分帶咗，不過有一頁留咗喺屋企。
Model: I brought most of it, but one page is at home.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use the insurer claims portal unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用保險公司索償網上平台。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我今日交齊啲資料，佢哋評估嗰陣咁我應該點做呀？我唔想又整多個問題出嚟。
Model: If I send everything today, what should I do while they assess it? I don’t want to create another problem.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about three to four weeks. While you wait, take reasonable steps to prevent further loss and keep evidence, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約三至四星期。等候期間，採取合理措施防止損失擴大，並保留證據，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果最後個決定對我不利，佢哋會唔會講明係申請邊一部分出問題？
Model: If the final answer goes against me, will they tell me what part of the application caused the problem?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, use internal dispute resolution, then AFCA if the complaint is eligible.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，先用內部爭議處理程序，如投訴合資格再向 AFCA 跟進。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返物品擁有證明呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use proof of ownership when I contact the service again.

## D093 — AFCA complaint
Topic: Insurance

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on an unresolved insurance complaint. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進未解決嘅保險投訴。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係最終回覆信喺八月八日收到。我擔心索償可能延誤，或者部分損失唔獲賠償。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that the final response letter arrived on 8 August. My concern is that the claim may be delayed or part of the loss may not be paid.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Right, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果到下星期五都未搞掂，會點㗎？我驚會錯過下一步。
Model: If this isn’t sorted out by next Friday, what happens? I’m worried I’ll miss the next step.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, prepare the final response and claim documents and check the AFCA complaint requirements. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，準備最終回覆同索償文件，並核實 AFCA 投訴要求。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有最終回覆、保單同索償文件。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will final response, policy and claim documents be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, Have you brought the records we just discussed?
Model: 好，頭先講嗰啲紀錄你有冇帶嚟？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白，主要紀錄有齊，仲有一份文件要下載返。
Model: Right, I have the main records. One document still needs to be downloaded.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through the AFCA online complaint service. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經AFCA 網上投訴服務提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我點樣先知佢哋真係收到啲文件，我想有個編號，之後跟進都有嘢可以講。
Model: How can I tell they’ve actually received the documents, I want something I can refer to if I need to follow it up.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That helps. I need to make a decision this week. Is there anything I should avoid doing while this is being checked?
Model: 咁就清楚喇。我今個星期要作決定。件事仲核對緊嗰陣，有冇啲嘢我應該避免做？

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 咁就清楚喇。先跟已經書面確認嘅資料做，亦要保留之後收到嘅文件同訊息，直到件事正式處理完。
Model: That helps. Use the information already confirmed in writing, and keep any new documents or messages until the matter is finalised.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, follow the complaint steps and information requested through the AFCA process.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，按 AFCA 程序所要求嘅投訴步驟同資料處理。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住澳洲金融投訴局，即係 AFCA。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Australian Financial Complaints Authority, or AFCA. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

## D094 — JobSeeker reporting
Topic: Social services

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain JobSeeker income reporting. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋JobSeeker 收入申報。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到今個申報期稅前收入六百二十澳元，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that earnings were $620 before tax this reporting period, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Sure, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 可以，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺今個月底之前處理。否則，我擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。
Model: I need to deal with it before the end of this month. Otherwise, I’m worried that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can I confirm that date is still correct?
Model: 我確認一下，嗰個日期而家仲啱？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係，呢個肯定係我手上最新嗰份資料。
Model: Yes, that’s definitely the latest detail I have.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Understood. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 明白。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有糧單同申報資料，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have pay slip and reporting details, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Sure, Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?
Model: 可以，相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 差唔多齊，我今晚可以攞埋欠嗰份。
Model: Nearly all. I can get the missing one this evening.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through your linked myGov service and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經你已連結嘅 myGov 服務上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 評估期間，仲有冇其他情況我應該早啲有準備？
Model: During the assessment, could anything else happen that I need to prepare for?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about seven to ten business days. Meanwhile, report required changes and keep evidence of what you submit.
Model: 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約七至十個工作日。期間，按要求申報變更，並保留已提交資料嘅證明。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果啲理由同實際發生嘅事對唔上，正確下一步係咩？我唔想不停打去唔同地方。
Model: If the reasons don’t match what actually happened, what’s the proper next step? I don’t want to keep ringing different people.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將JobSeeker 求職者補助金同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of JobSeeker Payment with the reference number for future contact.

## D095 — Rent Assistance update
Topic: Social services

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of updated rent details. I can see the notes from your enquiry. What’s the part you’re most concerned about?
Model: 我哋睇下更新租金資料嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，我睇到你之前個查詢紀錄。你而家最擔心係邊一部分呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話週租由四百七十加到五百一十澳元。我仍然擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。
Model: Earlier I was told rent increased from $470 to $510 a week. I’m still concerned that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: For this matter, we need to be clear about Rent Assistance. Remember that eligibility and payment rates depend on your circumstances and the information on record. Let me check how that applies to you.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係租金援助。要留意資格同付款金額會視乎你嘅情況同紀錄資料。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 去到通知所列日期之前，你可唔可以幫我睇吓仲欠唔欠資料？
Model: Before the date shown on the notice, can you check whether you still need anything from me?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗新租約或者加租通知。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have new lease or rent-increase notice. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use your linked myGov service, then save the online confirmation. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用你已連結嘅 myGov 服務提交，之後記低提交參考編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 啲文件交齊之後，我幾時問進度先唔算太早？
Model: Once the documents are in, when should I check the progress without chasing them too early?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about two weeks. During that period, report required changes and keep evidence of what you submit, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約兩星期。期間，按要求申報變更，並保留已提交資料嘅證明，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果佢哋未處理完之前我搬屋或者換電話，我係咪更新返而家個申請，定係等有決定先？
Model: If I change address or phone number before they finish, do I update the current request or wait for the decision first?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低租金援助，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down Rent Assistance so I can refer to the matter accurately.

## D096 — Child Care Subsidy hours
Topic: Social services

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good morning. You’re speaking with a Services Australia or community support officer. I’m calling about changed childcare use. I have your enquiry in front of me. Tell me what you’re hoping to sort out today.
Model: 早晨。呢度係Services Australia 或社區支援職員。我聯絡你係關於托兒使用情況變更。我而家見到你個查詢喇。你今日最想先搞清楚邊一樣嘢呀？

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我最擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。我想喺下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前處理好。
Model: My concern is that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly. I’d like this resolved before the next scheduled appointment or payment.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: What we need to look at is Child Care Subsidy, or CCS. eligibility and payment rates depend on your circumstances and the information on record. I’ll check the details on your case now.
Model: 呢件事最緊要先搞清楚托兒津貼，即係 CCS。資格同付款金額會視乎你嘅情況同紀錄資料。我幫你睇吓你個情況實際點樣套用。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我份文件寫住小朋友而家每星期返四日托兒。呢個資料我之前記低咗，但唔肯定而家仲係咪最新。你可唔可以幫我核實？
Model: My paperwork says the child now attends care four days a week. I wrote that down earlier, but I’m not sure it is still current. Could you check it?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The record matches that detail. Next, update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence. Check every date and number before submitting.
Model: 紀錄同呢個細節一致。下一步係更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。提交之前要核對清楚每個日期同數字。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗托兒註冊同活動資料。其中一份係電子版本。你哋要正本，定係清晰上載副本都可以？
Model: I have childcare enrolment and activity details. One item is electronic. Do you need originals, or are clear uploaded copies acceptable?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Unless your notice says otherwise, use your linked myGov service. Upload childcare enrolment and activity details, then save the receipt or reference.
Model: 除非通知另有指示，否則用你已連結嘅 myGov 服務。上載托兒註冊同活動資料，之後保存收據或者參考編號。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 咁就清楚喇。如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？
Model: That helps. If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That helps. Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.
Model: 咁就清楚喇。重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果個結果我點睇都覺得唔對路，係咪有正式嘅覆核程序可以跟？
Model: If the result still looks wrong to me, is there a formal review process I can use?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for written reasons and keep a dated record. After that, ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.
Model: 要求書面理由，並保留有日期嘅紀錄。之後，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 唔該晒。我會照住啲步驟做，之後收到新通知亦會睇清楚。
Model: Thanks for your help. I’ll follow those steps and check the next notice carefully.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay, Thanks. I’ll make sure I keep the receipt and a copy of the documents.
Model: 好，唔該。我會保存收據，同埋留返啲文件副本。

## D097 — Parenting Payment enquiry
Topic: Social services

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks for calling a Services Australia or community support officer. I can see an enquiry about a Parenting Payment enquiry. First, please confirm your name and the reference on the record.
Model: 多謝你打嚟Services Australia 或社區支援職員。我見到有一個關於Parenting Payment 查詢嘅查詢。首先請確認姓名同紀錄上嘅參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我今日收到更新，入面提到育兒補助金。我唔肯定呢個詞對我嘅情況有咩意思。
Model: I received an update today. It mentions Parenting Payment, and I’m not sure what that means for my situation.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: That term matters because eligibility and payment rates depend on your circumstances and the information on record. Your current record also says the separation date was 2 August.
Model: 呢個詞重要，因為資格同付款金額會視乎你嘅情況同紀錄資料。你目前紀錄亦顯示分居日期係八月二日。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 即係呢個細節仍然適用？我需要喺下星期五之前知道，因為款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。
Model: So that detail still applies? I need to know before next Friday, because my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes, that is what the current record shows. If it is wrong or incomplete, update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence.
Model: 係，現有紀錄係咁顯示。如果資料錯誤或者唔完整，就要更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗身份、收入同家庭資料。我要交邊部分？可唔可以網上提交，而唔使帶紙本？
Model: I’ve gathered identity, income and household details. Which parts should I send, and can I submit them online rather than bring paper copies?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Can you access those records right now?
Model: 你而家開唔開到嗰啲紀錄？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，大部分有呀，不過仲有一份喺屋企。
Model: Okay, I have most of them; one is still at home.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Use your linked myGov service unless your notice gives another method. Upload readable copies and keep the submission reference.
Model: 除非通知列明另一種方法，否則用你已連結嘅 myGov 服務。上載清晰副本，並保留提交參考編號。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我正式交咗資料之後，等緊期間有冇啲嘢一定要做或者唔好做呀？
Model: After I lodge the information, is there anything I should or shouldn’t do while I’m waiting?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The usual guide is about five business days. While you wait, report required changes and keep evidence of what you submit, and monitor any official messages.
Model: 一般參考時間係大約五個工作日。等候期間，按要求申報變更，並保留已提交資料嘅證明，並留意任何正式訊息。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我收到一個負面決定，我可唔可以先攞書面理由，再諗下一步？
Model: If I get a negative decision, can I get the reasons in writing before I decide what to do next?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Ask for the decision and reasons in writing. If the issue remains unresolved, ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.
Model: 可以。要求以書面提供決定同理由。如果問題仍然未解決，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好。我會將文件整理好，下次再聯絡服務機構時會用返育兒補助金呢個詞。
Model: All right. I’ll keep the documents together and use Parenting Payment when I contact the service again.

## D098 — Carer Payment enquiry
Topic: Social services

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: I’m following up on a carer-payment enquiry. Before we discuss the status, please confirm your full name and reference number for the file.
Model: 我係跟進照顧者補助查詢。傾進度之前，請確認你嘅全名同檔案參考編號。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我收到嘅資料係工作減到每星期十二個鐘。我擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。
Model: Thanks. The detail I was given is that work has reduced to twelve hours a week. My concern is that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Good, Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 好，你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我喺今個月底之前處理唔到，實際會有咩後果？
Model: What are the consequences if I can’t resolve this by the end of this month?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
