Source: Don’t assume the outcome. First, update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence. That gives the service the information needed to assess the matter.
Model: 唔好自行假設結果。首先，更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。咁樣服務機構先有需要嘅資料去評估。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有照顧資料、收入紀錄同醫療表格。呢啲夠唔夠開始處理，定係要等齊所有可能嘅證明先交？
Model: Will care details, income records and medical forms be enough to start, or should I wait until I have every possible supporting document?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Understood. Have you got the relevant paperwork there now?
Model: 明白。相關文件而家喺唔喺你度？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 主要嗰幾份有，一個檔案仲喺屋企部電腦。
Model: I have the main ones. One file is on my home computer.

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Start with the requested material through your linked myGov service. An officer can ask for more information if something relevant is missing.
Model: 先經你已連結嘅 myGov 服務提交已要求嘅資料。如果欠缺相關資料，職員可以再要求補充。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我正式提交之後，點知系統已經有紀錄，如果之後有爭議，嗰個日期可能好重要㗎。
Model: Once I lodge it, how will I know it’s on their system, That date could matter if there’s a dispute later.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Got it. If the details change before this is finalised, do I update them straight away or wait for someone to contact me?
Model: 明白。如果正式處理完之前資料有變，我應該即刻更新，定係等你哋聯絡我先？

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 明白。重要資料有變就按通知上面嘅渠道盡快申報，並保留確認紀錄。
Model: Got it. Report any important change promptly through the channel shown on your notice, and keep the confirmation for your records.

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Ask for a status update rather than duplicate the request. If you later dispute the outcome, ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.
Model: 應該查詢進度，而唔係重複提交。如果之後對結果有爭議，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會保留參考編號，亦會記住照顧者補助金。咁下次可以清楚解釋件事。
Model: Understood. I’ll keep the reference and remember Carer Payment. That should help me explain the issue clearly.

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Okay. I’ll bring the missing document when I have it and we can go from there.
Model: 好。我攞到欠嗰份文件就補返，到時再跟住處理。

## D099 — NDIS plan manager
Topic: Social services

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: You asked us to explain an NDIS plan-management question. I can do that after I confirm your full name and the reference number attached to the matter.
Model: 你之前要求我哋解釋NDIS 計劃管理問題。確認你嘅全名同相關參考編號之後，我可以同你講清楚。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係。我見到治療發票係二百四十五澳元，但唔明呢個細節會點影響我下一步要做嘅嘢。
Model: Yes. I can see that the therapy invoice is $245, but I don’t understand how that affects what I should do next.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Certainly. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 可以。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我需要喺通知所列日期之前處理。否則，我擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。
Model: I need to deal with it before the date shown on the notice. Otherwise, I’m worried that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: One last check on that point: has it changed at all?
Model: 呢一點我最後確認多次：之後有冇改過？

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 係，最新訊息都係咁講。
Model: Yes, the latest message says the same thing.

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Thanks. Could you explain what that means for my case, and what I need to do next?
Model: 唔該。你可唔可以講下呢個對我個案有咩影響，同埋我下一步要做咩？

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我有NDIS 計劃、服務協議同發票，但其中一個日期同舊紀錄唔同。提交文件時應唔應該解釋呢個差異？
Model: I have NDIS plan, service agreement and invoice, but one date differs from an older record. Should I explain that difference when I submit the documents?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Do you have the supporting documents with you today?
Model: 你今日有冇帶埋啲證明文件？

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 大部分都喺度，最後嗰份我今晚可以補交。
Model: Most are here; I can send the last one tonight.

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Upload the evidence through the plan manager’s invoice portal and briefly explain any genuine discrepancy. Keep the receipt or reference after submission.
Model: 應該。經計劃管理人嘅發票網上平台上載證明，並簡單解釋真實差異。提交後保留收據或者參考編號。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 佢哋未核對完份紀錄之前，有冇額外費用或者其他風險要預先預算？
Model: Before they finish reviewing the record, is there any extra fee or other risk I should budget for?

**S13 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Timing depends on the service and record. Allow about two weeks. Meanwhile, keep the invoice and service-delivery evidence while payment is being checked.
Model: 時間要視乎實際服務同紀錄。預大約兩星期。期間，付款核對期間保留發票同服務提供證明。

**S14 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果我睇完書面決定都仲係唔明，我要點樣跟正式程序提出覆核？
Model: If the written decision still doesn’t make sense, how do I challenge it through the right process?

**S15 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Keep the written decision and your evidence in date order. Then ask the plan manager to explain the invoice decision and the next available dispute step.
Model: 將書面決定同證據按日期整理好。之後，要求計劃管理人解釋發票決定同下一個可用爭議處理步驟。

**S16 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 多謝。我會照做，亦會將計劃管理人同參考編號一齊記低，方便之後聯絡。
Model: Thanks. I’ll do that and keep a note of plan manager with the reference number for future contact.

## D100 — Emergency relief referral
Topic: Social services

**S1 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Let’s check the progress of an emergency-relief request. Thanks for getting in touch. I’ve opened the matter here, so let’s go through what’s happened.
Model: 我哋睇下緊急援助要求嘅進度。為保障資料安全，我打開詳細紀錄之前，多謝你聯絡我哋。我而家開咗件紀錄，不如你講吓件事去到邊啦。

**S2 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 之前有人話下一筆收入五日後先收到。我仍然擔心款項或者支援可能延誤，或者計算錯。
Model: Earlier I was told the next income payment is due in five days. I’m still concerned that my payment or support may be delayed or calculated incorrectly.

**S3 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: For this matter, we need to be clear about emergency relief. Remember that eligibility and payment rates depend on your circumstances and the information on record. Let me check how that applies to you.
Model: 我見到嗰個紀錄。檔案上嘅關鍵用語係緊急生活援助。要留意資格同付款金額會視乎你嘅情況同紀錄資料。

**S4 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 如果有資料欠咗我想而家補。下一個已安排嘅預約或者付款日期之前仲有冇嘢要交？
Model: I’d rather fix any missing information now. Is there anything else you need before the next scheduled appointment or payment?

**S5 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: The next step is to update the relevant details and provide the requested evidence. The service can then check whether anything further is required.
Model: 下一步係更新相關資料，並提供所要求嘅證明。之後服務機構可以再核對係咪仲需要其他資料。

**S6 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 我已經準備咗有相身份證同現時財務資料。我而家可唔可以上載？提交之後會唔會有證明顯示日期同時間？
Model: I have photo ID and current financial information. Can I upload those now, and will I receive something proving the time and date of submission?

**S7 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Yes. Use your linked myGov service, then keep the submission receipt. Check the uploaded files are readable before you finish.
Model: 可以。用你已連結嘅 myGov 服務提交，之後保存確認編號。完成之前要檢查上載文件係咪清楚可讀。

**S8 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 上載完成之後，大概幾時再問吓會比較合理？
Model: After the upload is complete, what’s a sensible time to ask for an update?

**S9 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Allow about three to four weeks. During that period, report required changes and keep evidence of what you submit, and check the official contact channel regularly.
Model: 預大約三至四星期。期間，按要求申報變更，並保留已提交資料嘅證明，並定期查看正式聯絡渠道。

**S10 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 假如評估期間我有一項資料改咗，我係咪應該即刻通知佢哋？
Model: Suppose one of my details changes during the assessment, should I tell them straight away?

**S11 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese**
Source: Update any material change promptly and keep proof. If the final decision is disputed, ask for an explanation or formal review through the relevant service.
Model: 重要資料有變要盡快更新，並保留證明。如果對最終決定有爭議，經相關服務要求解釋或者正式覆核。

**S12 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English**
Source: 好，明白喇。我會將所有資料整理好，亦會記低緊急生活援助，等之後可以準確提及呢件事。
Model: That makes sense. I’ll keep everything together and write down emergency relief so I can refer to the matter accurately.
